*Harmon Bhasin,Kevin Flyangolts,Dianzhuo Wang,Evan Seeyave,Arjun Banerjee,Amanda Darling,Joshua Stallings,David Stern,Shawn Higdon,Claire Duvallet,Bryan Tegomoh,Kenny Workman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了BioSecBench-Surveillance基准测试，包含100个评估任务，用于测试AI代理是否能够从原始测序数据和监测背景中推断出正确的分析流程。最强配置（Opus 4.8 with PI和GPT-5.5 with Codex）仅达到约50%的正确率，表明当前AI在基因组监测分析方面仍不可完全信赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着病原体基因组监测规模的扩大，瓶颈正从数据生成转向数据分析。现有方法缺乏可验证的基准来评估AI代理能否像人类分析师一样，仅凭原始测序数据和监测背景信息推断出正确的分析流程。

Method: 构建了包含100个评估任务的BioSecBench-Surveillance基准测试，涵盖从分类学分类到基因工程检测等7个类别，涉及多种样本类型和测序技术。每个评估任务仅提供原始数据和监测背景，然后对AI代理的结构化回答进行确定性评分。

Result: 对16个模型-工具组合的3,962次可评分尝试进行评估，最强配置Opus 4.8 with PI达到50.2%（95% CI: 40.1-60.3%），GPT-5.5 with Codex同样为50.2%（95% CI: 40.8-59.6%），Opus 4.7 with PI为49.6%（95% CI: 40.0-59.2%），Sonnet 4.6 with PI为48.6%（95% CI: 38.9-58.3%）。即使代理调用了正确的工作流程，错误仍来自参数选择（如参考数据库、阈值、过滤器和标准化方法的选择）。

Conclusion: BioSecBench-Surveillance为衡量AI代理是否能在下一次疫情爆发时可信地执行基因组监测提供了标准化评估基准。当前最强AI模型的准确率仅约50%，远未达到可信赖的水平。

Abstract: As pathogen genomic surveillance scales, the bottleneck is shifting from data generation to analysis. We present BioSecBench-Surveillance, a verifiable benchmark of 100 evaluations testing whether AI agents can infer the right analysis pipeline from raw sequencing data and surveillance context. Each evaluation gives an agent only the data and context a human analyst would have, then grades its structured answer deterministically. The tasks span seven categories, from taxonomic classification to genetic-engineering detection, across diverse sample types and sequencing technologies. Across 3,962 gradable attempts from sixteen model-harness pairs, the strongest configuration cleared only about half. Opus 4.8 with PI led at 50.2 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 40.1 to 60.3 percent across 83 evaluations, tied with GPT-5.5 with Codex at 50.2 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 40.8 to 59.6 percent, followed by Opus 4.7 with PI at 49.6 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 40.0 to 59.2 percent, and Sonnet 4.6 with PI at 48.6 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 38.9 to 58.3 percent. Even when agents invoked the correct workflows, their mistakes came from the choices around them, such as which references, thresholds, filters, and normalization to apply. BioSecBench-Surveillance provides a standard for measuring whether agents can be trusted to perform genomic surveillance when the next outbreak arrives.

</details>


### [141] [Graph-Based Agentic AI with LangGraph: Workflow Pathways for Long-Running Stateful Business Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19297)
*Daniel Pearson,Sidney Shapiro,Emiliano Sebastian Gonzalez Venegas,Sanad Al-Khatib,Aurora Pinzón Arzola*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一份面向实践者的指南，介绍如何在长时间运行、有状态、多步骤的生成式AI业务流程中使用基于图的工作流路径。通过三个可执行示例（带修复循环的SQL分析、带证据门控的智能体检索增强生成、带中断和检查点恢复的人工参与策略审查），展示了类型化状态、条件路由、确定性工具、重试、中断、检查点和追踪的整合方式，并给出了LangGraph的适用性判断标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前业界对LangGraph等图编排框架存在过度追捧或误解，常将其视为模型质量基准工具。作者希望为实践者提供清晰的选择依据和实现模式，明确何时该使用LangGraph，何时更简单的替代方案（如ReAct、SDK循环、schema优先工具、DSPy）更为合适。

Method: 通过三个具体可执行配方（recipe）作为示例：1) 带修复循环的SQL分析；2) 带证据门控的智能体RAG；3) 带中断与检查点恢复的人工审核流程。作者从工作流复杂度匹配角度定位LangGraph，并与ReAct式循环、普通SDK循环、schema优先工具、DSPy等方案进行对比分析。

Result: 给出了LangGraph的适用边界：当工作流需要类型化状态、条件路由、确定性工具、重试/中断/检查点/追踪等结构时值得使用；对于基本工具使用，更简单的ReAct式或SDK循环更好；对于结构化提取和验证，schema优先工具更合适；当主要目标是提示或程序优化时，DSPy是更优选择。

Conclusion: LangGraph不是通用默认方案，而应基于工作流复杂度匹配来选择。正确的实现模式能让路由、暂停和审计追踪成为显式的产品行为，而非隐藏的提示逻辑。

Abstract: This paper is a practitioner guide to graph-based workflow pathways for long-running, stateful, multi-step generative AI systems in business processes. Rather than treating LangGraph, a low-level orchestration framework for stateful agents, as a model-quality benchmark target, we present three executable recipes -- SQL analytics with repair loops, agentic retrieval-augmented generation with evidence gating, and human-in-the-loop policy review with interrupt and checkpoint recovery -- to show how typed state, conditional routing, deterministic tools, retries, interrupts, checkpoints, and traces fit together. LangGraph is positioned by workflow-complexity fit, not as a universal default: simpler ReAct-style or plain SDK loops may be better for basic tool use, schema-first tools for structured extraction and validation, and DSPy when prompt or program optimization is the main goal. Each recipe explains when LangGraph is worth the extra structure and which implementation patterns make routes, pauses, and audit trails explicit product behavior rather than hidden prompt logic.

</details>


### [142] [LLM Detection as an Intervention: Downstream Impact under Strategic User Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19300)
*Meena Jagadeesan,Tatsunori Hashimoto,Jon Kleinberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过建立博弈模型揭示了一个反直觉现象：不完美的LLM检测器反而会激励用户增加LLM使用量，并可能导致输出质量下降，而检测到的属性则呈现先升后降的模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被广泛采用，人们越来越关注检测LLM生成的内容（如通过检测工具或语言模式启发式方法）。检测器作为一种干预手段，不仅影响被检测属性本身，还会影响下游指标如LLM使用量和输出质量。本文旨在揭示不完美的LLM检测器如何通过扭曲用户使用LLM的激励机制，对这些下游指标产生反直觉的影响。

Method: 作者开发了一个风格化模型，捕捉用户策略性地选择使用LLM的程度以及如何对内容进行后处理以降低被检测属性的行为。通过该模型分析检测器对用户行为、LLM使用量、输出质量以及被检测属性的影响，并在arXiv摘要的词频数据上进行实证验证。

Result: 1) LLM检测可能反直觉地导致人类增加LLM使用量；2) 即使降低被检测属性能够提升输出质量，引入LLM检测器也可能导致用户产出更低质量的输出；3) 被检测属性呈现清晰的"先升后降"模式，该模式在arXiv摘要的词频数据上得到实证复现。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了LLM检测如何扭曲LLM使用量和输出质量，发现了当LLM检测器作为下游指标的干预手段时存在的失效模式。

Abstract: As LLM adoption becomes more widespread, there is a growing interest in detecting LLM-generated content, for example through LLM detection tools and through heuristics based on language patterns. Detectors operate as an intervention that steers not only the detected attribute itself, but also downstream metrics such as LLM usage and output quality. In this work, we demonstrate how imperfect LLM detectors lead to counterintuitive impacts on these downstream metrics, by distorting how users are incentivized to use LLMs in their workflow. We develop a stylized model which captures how users strategically choose how much to use the LLM and how to post-process content to reduce the detected attribute. Using this model, we show that LLM detection can counterintuitively lead humans to increase their LLM usage. Moreover, even when reducing the detected attribute improves output quality, we find that introducing an LLM detector can lead users to produce lower quality outputs. In contrast, we show that detectors result in a clean "rise-then-fall" pattern for the detected attribute, which we empirically reproduce for word frequencies on arXiv abstracts. Altogether, our work illustrates how LLM detection can distort LLM usage and output quality, uncovering failure modes when LLM detectors operate as an intervention on these downstream metrics.

</details>


### [143] [ResearchArena: Evaluating Sabotage and Monitoring in Automated AI R&D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19321)
*Lena Libon,Ben Rank,Jehyeok Yeon,David Schmotz,Jeremy Qin,Daniel Donnelly,Derck Prinzhorn,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI控制方法，通过监控器检测AI agent在自动化AI研发中的隐蔽破坏行为。在ResearchArena框架的四个长期任务上评估发现，隐藏在训练数据中的破坏最难检测（不到一半成功率），仅靠监控器运行实验仍不足，需更深入的检测机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI agent开始自动化AI研发，需要一种安全评估方法确保其产出安全部署，即使agent本身不可信。现有方法缺乏对AI研发场景中隐蔽破坏行为的系统性检测方案。

Method: 提出AI控制方法，将agent视为潜在对手，使用监控器在部署前检测隐蔽破坏。构建ResearchArena框架，覆盖四个长期任务（安全后训练、能力后训练、CUDA内核优化、推理服务器优化），每个任务配对两种隐藏侧任务（嵌入式侧任务破坏产出工件本身，独立侧任务在沙箱中执行隐蔽越界操作）。评估前沿agent的破坏与监控能力，监控器沿两个轴变化：是否可执行并探测产出工件，是否查看agent的思维链。

Result: 隐藏在训练数据中的破坏最难检测，被标记率不到一半。允许监控器运行实验有助于检测，但不足以完全捕获：监控器仍可能因仅检查表面、注意到异常但将其合理化、或用错误测试探测工件而遗漏嵌入式破坏。

Conclusion: ResearchArena作为模块化框架发布，用于评估自动AI研发中的破坏与安全控制。

Abstract: As AI agents begin to automate AI R&D, we need ways to assess whether their outputs are safe to deploy, even when the agents themselves may be untrusted. AI control offers one such approach: rather than trusting the agent, it treats it as a potential adversary and uses a monitor to detect covert sabotage before deployment. We evaluate AI control for automated AI R&D with ResearchArena, a framework spanning four long-horizon tasks: safety post-training, capabilities post-training, CUDA-kernel optimization, and inference-server optimization. Because the deliverable in AI R&D is an artifact that will be deployed and run, we pair each main task with two kinds of hidden side task: an embedded side task that sabotages the submitted model, adapter, kernel, or server itself, and an independent side task that takes a covert out-of-scope action in the sandbox while leaving the artifact honest. We evaluate frontier agents at both sabotage and monitoring, across four monitor types that vary along two axes: whether the monitor may execute and probe the produced artifact, and whether it sees the agent's chain-of-thought. We find that sabotage hidden in the training data is the hardest to catch, flagged fewer than half the time. Letting the monitor run experiments on the artifact, rather than only read the trajectory, helps, but it is not enough: monitors still miss embedded sabotage by inspecting only the surface, by noticing the anomaly but explaining it away, or by probing the artifact with the wrong test. We release ResearchArena as a modular framework for evaluating sabotage and control in automated AI R&D.

</details>


### [144] [Associative Emotional Learning in Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19327)
*Seowung Leem,Andreas Keil,Mingzhou Ding,Ruogu Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种深度神经网络模型来模拟视觉效价加工的联想情绪学习，该模型包含视觉编码模块和情绪效价识别模块。通过在新型巴甫洛夫学习范式上的训练，模型成功复现了人类联想学习中的联结形成和泛化等行为特征，并在单神经元和群体水平上观察到条件刺激与非条件刺激神经表征的逐渐对齐。与人类实验数据的比较进一步验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算模型（如Rescorla-Wagner模型）在解释联想情绪学习方面存在局限，尤其是在应用于神经数据时。为了突破这些限制，作者探索利用深度神经网络这一新途径来建模联想情绪学习过程。

Method: 构建了一个由视觉模块（编码复杂自然场景）和效价识别模块（识别情绪效价）组成的深度神经网络模型，并在一种新型巴甫洛夫学习范式上对模型进行测试。

Result: 模型成功复现了人类联想学习研究的多个观察结果，包括联结形成和泛化；在单单元和神经群体水平上，条件刺激和非条件刺激的神经表征逐渐对齐；与人类实验数据的比较进一步验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 深度神经网络模型结合适当的学习算法，可用于模拟联想情绪/效价学习的行为和神经特征。

Abstract: Associative emotional learning enables organisms to adaptively link pleasant or unpleasant outcomes to the presence of predictive stimuli. Whereas computational models such as the Rescorla-Wagner model have shed light on this important function, the limitations of these models are also known, especially when they are applied to neural data. The advent of deep neural networks has opened another avenue for modeling associative emotional learning. In this work we proposed a deep neural network model of visual valence processing, consisting of a visual module that encodes complex natural scenes and a module that recognizes their emotional significance in terms of valence, a key dimension of emotion, and tested a novel Pavlovian learning paradigm on the model. The results showed that with learning, the model reproduced several observations from human associative learning studies, including association formation and generalization, and that the neural representations of the conditioned and the unconditioned stimuli became increasingly aligned both at the single unit and at the neural population level. Comparison between the model and human experimental data provided further validation of our approach. This study thus suggests that deep neural network models, when combined with appropriate learning algorithms, can be used to model behavioral and neural signatures of associative emotion/valence learning.

</details>


### [145] [Agents in the Wild: Where Research Meets Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19336)
*Grace Hui Yang,Pranav N. Venkit,Hooman Sedghamiz,Enrico Santus,Victor Dibia,Ioana Baldini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本教程探讨了基于大语言模型(LLM)的智能体系统从研究原型到生产部署的转变，重点关注推理规划、多智能体协调和评估方面的进展，并通过制药发现和金融系统的案例研究，分析成功设计模式及安全可靠性部署的缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管学术界已强调基准测试和算法创新，但智能体系统在生产部署中面临鲁棒性、安全性和可靠性等新挑战，亟需对这些问题进行系统性探讨。

Method: 通过案例研究（制药发现和金融系统）分析常见设计模式，探讨推理与规划、多智能体协调和评估方面的进展，并讨论实际缓解策略如验证管线、回退机制和人机协同监督。

Result: 总结了使智能体系统成功的设计模式，以及针对不同故障模式的实用缓解策略，为跨行业的安全可靠部署提供了设计模式、评估清单和模板。

Conclusion: 智能体系统正处于从研究向生产部署过渡的关键阶段，需要关注部署中出现的鲁棒性、安全性和可靠性挑战，通过系统化的设计模式和缓解策略实现安全可靠的行业应用。

Abstract: Agentic systems large language model (LLM) based architectures capable of reasoning, planning, acting, and coordinating with tools and other agents are rapidly transitioning from research prototypes to production scale deployments across domains such as software engineering, scientific discovery, and finance. While academic work has emphasized benchmarks and algorithmic innovation, deployment raises new challenges around robustness, safety, and reliability. This tutorial brings together researchers and practitioners to explore advances in reasoning and planning, multi agent coordination, and evaluation, highlighting open challenges arising from deployment experience. Through applied case studies in pharmaceutical discovery and financial systems, we analyze common design patterns that make agentic systems successful, and discuss practical mitigation strategies for failure modes, such as verification pipelines, fallback mechanisms, and human in the loop supervision. Attendees will gain a comprehensive view of the field along with concrete design patterns, evaluation checklists, and templates for safe and reliable deployment across industries.

</details>


### [146] [CodeRescue: Budget-Calibrated Recovery Routing for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19338)
*Qijia He,Jiayi Cheng,Chenqian Le,Rui Wang,Xunmei Liu,Yixian Chen,Jie Mei,Zhihao Wang,Xupeng Chen,Yuhuan Chen,Tao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向代码智能体的后失败恢复路由策略，通过监督学习和保形风险控制（CRC）层，在预算约束下动态决定是使用廉价模型进行恢复还是升级到昂贵模型，在GPT-5.4-nano/GPT-5.4设置中以35%的平均恢复成本达到甚至超越始终升级策略的解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成本感知系统通常采用级联决策（先用廉价模型，失败后升级到昂贵模型），但在代码执行场景中，执行反馈使得廉价模型仍有恢复价值。因此需要回答：何时应继续使用廉价计算资源，何时应升级？

Method: 将后失败决策形式化为异构动作上的恢复路由问题；从执行轨迹中训练一个有监督路由器；引入保形风险控制（CRC）层，在不重新训练的情况下选择部署时的代价惩罚项，并在可交换性假设下提供边际期望成本控制。

Result: 在五个代码基准测试的留出失败样本上，廉价恢复与升级表现出互补的成功模式。校准后的前沿优于固定动作、纯提示路由器和二元级联基线；在GPT-5.4-nano/GPT-5.4设置中，CRC校准前沿点以35%的平均恢复成本达到超过始终升级的解决率。

Conclusion: 提出的恢复路由与CRC校准方法能够在不同预算下有效平衡代码智能体的恢复成本与解决率，实现了更经济的预算分配。

Abstract: Coding agents increasingly operate in executable environments where a failed attempt produces actionable feedback rather than merely an incorrect answer. Existing cost-aware systems typically treat such failures as cascade decisions: try a cheap model first, then escalate hard cases to a stronger and more expensive model. In coding, however, execution feedback can also make further cheap-model recovery worthwhile, raising a budgeted deployment question: when should an agent spend more cheap compute, and when should it escalate? We formulate this post-failure decision as recovery routing over heterogeneous actions and train a supervised router from execution rollouts. To make the same router usable under changing budgets, we add a Conformal Risk Control (CRC) layer that selects a deployment-time cost penalty without retraining and provides marginal expected-cost control under exchangeability. Across held-out failures from five coding benchmarks, cheap recovery and escalation exhibit complementary success patterns. The calibrated frontier improves over fixed actions, prompt-only routers, and a binary cascade baseline; in the main GPT-5.4-nano/GPT-5.4 setting, one CRC-calibrated frontier point exceeds always-escalate solve rate while using 35% of its mean recovery cost. Code is available at https://github.com/Qijia-He/agent-budget-control.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [FARO: Feasibility-Aware Robot Motion Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18362)
*Michal Ciebielski,Shafeef Omar,Aaron Johnson,Majid Khadiv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种嵌套运动动力学框架，结合可行性引导的树搜索和大语言模型接触计划采样策略，用于快速检查人形机器人全身操作行为的可行性并生成动态一致的轨迹，所生成轨迹可通过强化学习控制器执行于真实场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在未见场景中快速规划新颖行为仍面临挑战，其高维、混合、欠驱动的特性阻碍了全身操作的实时规划与可行性验证。

Method: 提出嵌套运动动力学框架实现快速可行性检查与动态一致轨迹生成；集成该模块到可行性引导树搜索中；利用大语言模型（LLM）进行接触计划采样策略。

Result: 该框架显著改进了搜索过程；生成的轨迹能够被强化学习（RL）控制器跟踪，并具备在真实场景中执行的高质量。

Conclusion: 嵌套运动动力学框架结合LLM引导的接触采样和树搜索，可有效支持人形机器人全身操作在真实场景中的快速规划与执行。

Abstract: Fast planning of novel behaviors in unseen scenarios remains a fundamental challenge in robotics. The high-dimensional, hybrid, and underactuated nature of humanoid loco-manipulation continues to hinder the realization of this goal. In this paper, we address this challenge by proposing a nested kino-dynamic framework for rapid feasibility checking and dynamically consistent trajectory generation given a candidate contact sequence. By integrating this module with a feasibility-guided tree search and a Large Language Model (LLM)-based contact plan sampling strategy, we demonstrate that the proposed framework can substantially improve the search process. Furthermore, we show that the generated trajectories can be tracked using a reinforcement learning (RL)-based controller and show that the resulting trajectories are of sufficiently high quality for execution in real-world loco-manipulation scenarios. A supplementary video is available at: https://youtu.be/R6qCHoCormQ.

</details>


### [148] [Towards Torque-Driven Reinforcement Learning for Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18365)
*Jordan Dowdy,Jean Chagas Vaz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种不需要速度估计和外感传感器的强化学习扭矩控制框架，成功应用于重型高扭矩四足机器人Unitree B1，实现了在复杂地形上的高速运动和 stair 攀爬能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于位置的强化学习框架对地形适应性差，依赖状态估计技术（如线速度估计），且多用于轻量级小型四足机器人，难以应用于高复杂度任务的重型高扭矩四足机器人。

Method: 开发了一种基于强化学习的扭矩控制框架，无需智能体当前速度作为输入，利用Nvidia Isaac Sim和Isaac Lab仿真环境在Unitree B1四足机器人上进行训练和验证。

Result: 在Unitree B1上达到3.5 m/s的线速度和1.5 rad/s的角速度，且无需外感传感器即可上下楼梯。

Conclusion: 所提出的强化学习扭矩控制框架有效克服了传统方法的局限性，能够在无需速度估计和外感传感器的条件下，实现重型高扭矩四足机器人在复杂地形上的高效运动控制。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for legged robots is advancing locomotion, demonstrating its ability to adapt to new and challenging terrain. Traditionally, these RL locomotion frameworks are position-based, making the policy less adaptable to terrain types and requiring state estimation techniques in the observation space, i.e., linear velocity. Moreover, these RL frameworks often use small, lightweight quadrupeds that are limited in their viability for high-complexity tasks due to hardware constraints. This work explores an RL torque control framework for heavyweight high-torque quadrupeds. The RL framework in this paper can traverse rough terrain and effectively track a desired linear velocity without requiring knowledge of the agent's current velocity. Using Nvidia's Isaac Sim and Isaac Lab, simulation results of the RL torque control policy are shown on the Unitree B1 quadruped, achieving speeds of 3.5 m/s and 1.5 rad/s. In addition, the quadruped can walk up and down stairs without the aid of an exteroceptive sensor.

</details>


### [149] [The Open Ant: A Robot Platform for Reinforcement Learning Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18488)
*Elena Sorina Lupu,Patrick Spieler,Khurram Javed,Kris De Asis,John D. Martin,Martha Steenstrup,Joseph Modayil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Open Ant，一个基于常见Gymnasium Ant环境的物理机器人平台，支持从零开始直接学习行走策略（约1小时），以及仿真到现实的迁移学习。该平台具有易于修复、新用户快速上手等特点，已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强化学习研究主要基于仿真环境，导致算法从仿真迁移到物理现实时存在不确定性。为解决这一问题，需要设计一个能够简化从仿真到物理过渡的实验平台。

Method: 设计了Open Ant物理平台（对应Gymnasium Ant仿真环境），分别使用SARSA(λ)和SAC两种算法从物理机器人的直接经验中学习行走策略，并验证了仿真到现实的策略迁移能力。同时评估了平台的可修复性、更新便捷性以及新用户上手速度。

Result: 两种不同RL算法（SARSA(λ)和SAC）均可在大约1小时内从物理机器人经验中学习到有效的行走策略；仿真中学习的策略可成功迁移到物理平台；新用户能快速上手，平台易于修复和更新。

Conclusion: Open Ant能够有效帮助RL研究人员将仿真环境的实验扩展到物理机器人实验，降低从仿真到现实的迁移门槛，建议使用仿真环境的RL研究者采用该平台将机器人实验纳入评估体系。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) research has demonstrated success in both physical and simulated domains; however, the predominant methodology remains rooted in simulations. The predominance of simulations makes translating research to physical reality uncertain for both algorithms and researchers. We propose a physical platform that is designed to simplify the transition. In this paper, we present the Open Ant: a physical variant of the commonly used Gymnasium Ant environment, along with a simulation. We demonstrate that competent walking policies can be learned from scratch in approximately one hour directly from the physical robot's experience for two substantially different RL algorithms: SARSA($λ$) and Soft Actor-Critic (SAC). Separately, we show policies that were learned in simulation transfer to reality. We also examine how well the platform supports a nimble experimental ecosystem. Specifically, we observe the speed with which new users from diverse backgrounds achieve their first success with the platform, and how easily the platform can be repaired and updated when hardware issues arise. Both the hardware design and software are available as open-source on GitHub for ease of customization. In summary, we advocate for the use of the Open Ant for RL researchers who frequently use simulated environments, so they can more easily include robot experiments in their evaluations.

</details>


### [150] [Beyond Fixed Goal Delivery: Online POMDP Planning for Target Interception in Crowds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18517)
*Himanshu Gupta,Kelvin Aladum,Nisar Ahmed,Bradley Hayes,Zachary Sunberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在拥挤环境中机器人拦截移动目标的问题，将其建模为部分可观察马尔可夫决策过程(POMDP)，并通过在线树搜索求解。对比了两种规划器：先规划路径再调制速度的顺序路径-速度规划器，以及转向和速度联合分支的统一规划器。实验表明，在高密度人群中，统一规划器的安全拦截率比顺序规划器高31个百分点，耗时少44%，揭示了空间限制的结构性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在拥挤环境中拦截移动目标时，人类导航意图不可直接观测，机器人需要推理多种可能的未来交互结果。现有方法在规划空间结构对计算效率和拦截性能的影响方面理解不足，特别是顺序式路径-速度规划与联合式转向-速度规划的差异。

Method: 将拦截问题建模为POMDP，采用固定计算预算下的在线树搜索求解。对比两种动作空间结构：1) 顺序路径-速度规划器（先规划空间路径，再沿路径调制速度）；2) 统一规划器（在树搜索中联合分支转向和速度）。在至多200人的仿真场景中进行了受控比较实验。

Result: 低密度人群下两种方法表现相似，但随密度增加性能差异显著。最高密度下，顺序规划器安全拦截率比统一规划器低31个百分点，所需时间多44%。

Conclusion: 动作空间结构对树搜索的计算分配和最终性能有决定性影响。顺序式路径-速度规划在高密度人群中存在空间限制的结构性缺陷，而联合式转向-速度规划能更有效地分配计算资源，实现更好的拦截性能。

Abstract: Target interception in crowded environments requires reaching a moving objective while navigating among multiple uncertain human agents. Since human navigation intent is not directly observable, the robot must reason over multiple possible future interaction outcomes. We formulate interception in crowds as a partially observable Markov decision process and solve it online using tree search under a fixed computational budget. In this setting, the action-space structure directly shapes the search tree and how computational effort is allocated. We perform a controlled comparison between a sequential path-speed planner, which first plans a spatial path and then modulates speed along it, and a unified planner that jointly branches over steering and speed within tree search. Across simulations with up to 200 humans, both approaches perform similarly at low crowd density but diverge sharply as density increases. At the highest crowd density, the sequential planner has a safe-interception rate 31 percentage points lower and requires 44% more time than the unified steering-speed planner, revealing a structural limitation of spatial restriction. Project webpage: https://tic-planning.github.io/

</details>


### [151] [DASH Robot: Minimalistic Design and Optimal Aerial-Terrestrial Locomotion via Contact-Implicit Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18527)
*Ryan Gomes Paiva,Conrad Ho,Jiarong Kang,Kunzhao Ren,Xiangru Xu,Xiaobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DASH（导管式空中弹簧跳跃机器人）的空中-地面两栖机器人，通过将导管风扇同轴体与底部弹簧腿有机集成，实现了机械简单、运动灵活且能效高的空中飞行和地面弹跳，并采用接触隐式模型预测控制器实现两种模式的最优自动切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中-地面两栖机器人通常机械结构复杂，难以同时兼顾空中敏捷飞行和地面高效运动。本文旨在设计一种机械结构极简、运动模式多样且能效高的统一移动机器人。

Method: 采用导管风扇同轴体作为主要推力矢量致动器实现敏捷飞行，在底部集成轻量化弹簧腿实现地面弹跳及能量循环；利用接触隐式模型预测控制器自动选择最优运动模式和致动策略。

Result: 成功验证了DASH在周期性弹跳、空中飞行以及无需显式模式切换的障碍穿越等任务中的表现，证明了设计和控制的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的有机集成设计和接触隐式控制方法能够在极简机械结构下实现空中-地面两栖机器人的高效、灵活运动，为未来多功能移动机器人提供了新思路。

Abstract: We present a novel and minimalistic design of an aerial-terrestrial robot DASH: Ducted Aerial Spring Hopper. The goal is to enable both aerial and ground locomotion capabilities on a unified mobile robot that is mechanically-minimalistic, locomotion-versatile, and energy-efficient. We propose an organic integration of ducted fan co-axial body with a springy leg at the bottom for realization. The ducted fan module provides thrust-vectoring as the main actuation for agile flying; when it is combined with the light-weight spring leg, the robot realizes highly efficient ground hopping with energy circulation. Moreover, to realize optimal locomotion with two modes, we employ a contact-implicit model predictive controller to automatically choose locomotion modes and actuation. We successfully validated the design and control of DASH through a range of tasks, including periodic hopping, aerial flight, and mode-free locomotion with autonomous mode transitions during obstacle traversal.

</details>


### [152] [Two-Stage Extrinsic Calibration of a Static Line-Scanning Lidar with a Rotary Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18578)
*Vikram Shree,Hike Danakian,Long Nguyen,Rajanish Gokidi,Patrick Nercessian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了旋转平台上线扫描激光雷达的旋转轴标定问题，提出了静态和动态两种自动估计算法，并在真实数据集上验证了其收敛特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转平台上的线扫描激光雷达需要通过相对运动构建3D点云，但旋转轴与激光雷达坐标系之间的变换关系未知且直接影响点云重建质量，因此需要精确标定旋转轴。

Method: 提出了自动化的静态和动态两种方法来估计旋转平台旋转轴相对于静态线扫描激光雷达的变换关系。

Result: 在自定义旋转平台和FMCW激光雷达的真实数据集上验证了算法，并研究了不同初始条件下的收敛特性。

Conclusion: 所提出的自动标定方法能够有效估计旋转轴变换，对提升3D点云重建质量具有重要意义。

Abstract: A line-scanning lidar yields range and azimuth values in a fixed plane. To perceive surrounding objects in 3D, there must be relative motion between the lidar plane and the object. Thus, using a rotating base-platform is promising for industrial applications where objects need to be scanned or inspected precisely, and is the main focus of this work. In the rotary platform setup, a 3D point cloud of an object can be constructed if the axis of rotation and the precise motion about that axis are known. However, this setup gives rise to the following problem: how can the axis of rotation of the platform be accurately identified with respect to the lidar coordinate system? It is referred to as the calibration problem in the robotics community. Any inaccuracy in this transformation directly affects the quality of the reconstructed point cloud, leading to misrepresentation of the object of interest. In this work, we explore automated approaches to statically and dynamically estimate the transformation of a rotary platform's axis of rotation with respect to a static line-scanning lidar. The proposed algorithms have been validated on real-world datasets obtained from a custom made rotary platform and an FMCW lidar, and their convergence characteristics are studied for various initial conditions.

</details>


### [153] [STeP: Signal Temporal Logic for Precise Specifications for Action Generation with Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18580)
*Kasra Torshizi,Anukriti Singh,Sidharth Mathur,Khuzema Habib,Leo Du,Pratap Tokekar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种分层框架，利用信号时序逻辑（STL）作为高层语言理解与低层机器人执行之间的共享表示，将自然语言指令分解为子任务并生成STL规格，通过STL引导的模型预测控制或监控学习策略执行，从而提升语言条件机器人规划的精度、可靠性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型虽然泛化能力强，但缺乏可解释性，且难以精确遵循包含空间、时间和逻辑约束的自然语言指令。

Method: 构建分层框架：高层策略利用VLM将语言指令分解为子任务，为每个子任务生成STL规格并选择低层策略；低层通过STL引导的MPC直接强制执行约束，或监控学习策略的执行；将STL集成到计划验证、子任务监控和重新规划中。

Result: 在真实桌面场景中验证了该方法，表明形式化规格能提升语言条件机器人规划的精度、可靠性和可解释性。

Conclusion: STL作为共享表示能有效桥接高层语言理解与低层执行，通过形式化约束提升机器人任务规划的鲁棒性和可解释性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown impressive generalization, but often lack interpretability and can struggle to follow precise natural language instructions that encode spatial, temporal, and logical requirements. We propose a hierarchical framework that uses Signal Temporal Logic (STL) as a shared representation connecting high-level language understanding with low-level robot execution. A high-level policy leverages a VLM to decompose language instructions into high-level subtasks, generate STL specifications for each subtask, and choose a low-level policy for executing each subtask. The STL specifications translate language-derived intent into precise constraints, and the low-level policy selection determines whether those constraints are enforced directly through STL-guided model-predictive control or monitored during execution of a learned policy for perceptually complex, or contact-rich behaviors. By integrating STL into plan validation, low-level policy, subtask monitoring, and replanning, our framework enables language-derived plans to be checked, optimized, and revised at runtime using a common formal structure. We evaluate the approach on a real-world tabletop domain, demonstrating how formal specifications can improve the precision, reliability, and interpretability of language-conditioned robot planning.

</details>


### [154] [Bridging the Sim-to-Real Gap under Real-Time Constraints in Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18586)
*Hossein Maghsoumi,Yaser P. Fallah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文从全栈实时系统角度分析了自动驾驶赛车领域中的sim-to-real迁移问题，提出了物理/计算/执行三层分析框架，给出了诊断指标和缓解策略，并制定了可复现的基准测试指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶赛车在高速、窄稳定性边界和严格实时约束的极端条件下，sim-to-real差距尤为显著——仿真中表现优异的控制器在实物平台上常因动力学偏差、估计延迟和执行延迟的交互作用而性能骤降。现有工作缺乏对跨层失配传播机制的系统性理解。

Method: 提出结构化三层视角（物理层/计算层/执行层）分析失配在闭环反馈中的传播与放大；引入性能翻转、稳定性导向指标、延迟与噪声敏感性、延迟分布特征等诊断指标；从部署导向视角综合缓解策略，强调执行感知与延迟感知设计；制定考虑计算与时间约束的可复现基准测试指南。

Result: 建立了跨层失效机制的分析框架，明确了失配如何通过闭环反馈传播放大；提供了超越单圈时间的多维度诊断指标；给出了执行感知与延迟感知的缓解策略；制定了兼顾计算与时间约束的公平可复现评估基准。

Conclusion: 该框架揭示了跨层失效机理，为在动态极限附近运行的可部署自动驾驶赛车系统提供了实用设计原则。

Abstract: Autonomous racing exposes the sim-to-real gap under extreme operating conditions characterized by high speed, tight stability margins, and stringent real-time constraints. Although simulation is indispensable for development, controllers that perform well in simulation often degrade abruptly on physical platforms due to interacting effects of dynamics mismatch, estimation delay, and execution-layer latency. This paper frames sim-to-real transfer in autonomous racing as a full-stack, real-time systems problem. We introduce a structured three-layer perspective (Physical/Cyber/Execution) to analyze how mismatches propagate and amplify through closed-loop feedback. We present diagnostic metrics beyond nominal lap time, including performance flip, stability-oriented measures, sensitivity to delay and noise, and latency distribution characterization. Mitigation strategies are synthesized from a deployment-oriented viewpoint, emphasizing execution-aware and delay-aware design. Finally, we outline benchmarking guidelines that enable reproducible and fair sim-to-real evaluation under compute and timing constraints. The resulting framework clarifies cross-layer failure mechanisms and provides practical design principles for deployable autonomous racing systems operating near dynamic limits.

</details>


### [155] [Intelligent Multi-UAV Navigation in ITNTNs: A Hierarchical LLM Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18604)
*Zijiang Yan,Hao Zhou,Wael Jaafar,Jianhua Pei,Ping Wang,Halim Yanikomeroglu,Hina Tabassum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种分层LLM驱动控制框架，云端大LLM（部署在HAPS上）负责慢时间尺度全局负载均衡，边缘轻量LLM将局部观测翻译为战术子目标，再由快速DRL控制器执行无碰撞、感知切换的轨迹，显著降低碰撞率并提升系统吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速无人机在3D空中高速公路部署需要协调物理飞行运动学与多层网络切换，现有DRL缺乏零样本策略推理能力，而LLM推理延迟高不适用于实时气动控制，因此需要弥合两者之间的差距。

Method: 提出分层LLM驱动控制框架：云端大LLM（部署于HAPS）负责慢时间尺度全局负载均衡，边缘轻量LLM将局部观测转化为战术子目标，快速时间尺度物理DRL控制器执行无碰撞、感知切换的轨迹。

Result: 仿真结果表明该智能体架构显著降低了碰撞率并提高了系统聚合吞吐量。

Conclusion: 分层LLM-DRL框架能够有效结合LLM的语义推理能力与DRL的实时控制能力，解决高速无人机在异构网络中的协调问题。

Abstract: The deployment of high-speed Uncrewed Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) in 3D aerial highways necessitates robust coordination of physical flight kinematics and multi-tier network handovers. While Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) offers rapid tactical control, it lacks the zero-shot strategic reasoning required to quickly adapt to dynamic Integrated Terrestrial and Non-Terrestrial Networks (ITNTNs). Conversely, Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at semantic reasoning but suffer from high inference latency, rendering them unsuitable for real-time aerodynamic control. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel Hierarchical LLM-driven control framework. A massive cloud-based LLM deployed on a High-Altitude Platform Station (HAPS) manages slow-timescale global load balancing, while lightweight edge-LLMs on individual UAVs translate local observations into tactical sub-goals. These sub-goals guide a fast-timescale physical DRL controller to execute collision-free, handover-aware trajectories. Simulation results demonstrate that our agentic architecture significantly reduces collision rates and improves aggregate system throughput compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [156] [On the Limits of Sampling-Based Reachability: Geometry, Dynamics, and Sample Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18606)
*Jixian Liu,Ihab Tabbara,Hussein Sibai,Enrique Mallada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了基于采样的可达集恢复问题，将其建模为几何支撑估计，证明了在初始集补集的正可达性和动力系统的Lipschitz连续性下，概率覆盖保证可提升为Hausdorff距离精度，给出了样本复杂度上界和下界，揭示了维度与时间跨度的指数依赖是本质性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样方法虽在可达性分析中展现潜力并提供有限样本保证，但初始集几何结构、动力学特性及采样分布如何影响估计精度的理论分析尚不完善，缺乏系统的几何与概率结合的刻画。

Method: 将采样可达集恢复问题转化为几何支撑估计问题，结合初始集补集的正可达性（positive reach）与动力学Lipschitz连续性两个正则性条件，建立概率覆盖保证向Hausdorff距离精度的提升关系，推导样本复杂度上界，并通过极小极大下界证明优化结果的本质紧性。

Result: 1) 正可达性与Lipschitz动力学共同保证恢复问题的良定性；2) 上界样本复杂度为$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\big((e^{3LT}/r)^n\big)$；3) 极小极大下界为$\Omega\big((e^{LT}/r)^n\big)$，指数依赖维度和时间跨度为本质性，不可消除。

Conclusion: 采样可达集恢复中维度诅咒和时间跨度的指数衰减是内在的而非方法缺陷，对抗采样仅能改善常数，无法改变量级缩放规律。

Abstract: Reachability analysis is central to safety-critical control, robotics, and neural network verification, but classical computational methods, such as Hamilton--Jacobi reachability and set propagation, scale poorly with state dimension. Sampling-based methods have emerged as a promising alternative, often providing finite-sample guarantees that bound the probability-mass left uncovered. However, an explicit account of how the geometry of the initial set, the dynamics, and the sampling law affect the accuracy of the estimator is not fully available in the literature. We study this by casting sampling-based reachable-set recovery as geometric support estimation over a family of problems specified by an initial set, its dynamics, and a sampling law. First, we identify two regularity properties, positive reach of the initial set's complement and Lipschitz continuity of the dynamics, that together make recovery well-posed: a probability-mass coverage guarantee can be upgraded to accuracy $r$ in Hausdorff distance. Second, we bound the resulting sample complexity: recovery is achievable with $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\big((e^{3LT}/r)^n\big)$ samples, exponential in both the state dimension and the time horizon. Third, we show that neither can be removed: an minimax lower bound of $Ω\big((e^{LT}/r)^n\big)$ holds for every estimator, so the exponential dependence on dimension and the degradation over the horizon are both intrinsic, not artifacts of a particular method. Experiments on nonlinear systems confirm that adversarial sampling improves constants but not the scaling.

</details>


### [157] [End-to-end Conditional Diffusion for Realistic and Controllable Visual Traffic Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18637)
*Jingzheng Li,Yufei Ge,Zhijun Chen,Qianren Mao,Zizhe Wang,Binhang Qi,Bing Li,Keyu Chen,Baochang Zhang,Xianglong Liu,Philip S Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出E2E-CDiff，一种端到端条件扩散框架，用于生成既真实又可控的闭环交通场景，特别是在安全关键交互场景下。该框架联合去噪未来运动状态和可执行低层控制，通过可微引导实现可控性与真实性的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的方法难以平衡可控性和真实性：要么缺乏对交通行为的细粒度控制，要么以牺牲行为合理性为代价换取可控性。传统两阶段轨迹-控制器管线存在规划-控制不匹配问题。

Method: E2E-CDiff是一个端到端条件扩散框架，以前视视觉观测为条件，联合去噪未来运动状态和可执行低层控制信号。采用可微引导来调节速度、强制可行驶区域合规，并支持碰撞避免或碰撞寻求行为。

Result: 在Bench2Drive数据集上，E2E-CDiff相比代表性的强化学习和模仿学习基线实现了更优的可控性-真实性权衡；其碰撞引导变体能够诱导具有挑战性的交互场景；作为自车规划器也表现出竞争力。

Conclusion: 端到端状态-动作扩散框架能够有效解决可控性与真实性的平衡问题，且具有作为通用规划器的泛化能力。

Abstract: Generating closed-loop traffic scenarios that are both realistic and controllable is crucial for evaluating autonomous driving systems, especially under rare safety-critical interactions. Existing learning-based methods often struggle to balance controllability and realism, offering either limited fine-grained control over traffic behavior or controllable scenarios at the expense of behavioral plausibility. This paper presents E2E-CDiff, an end-to-end conditional diffusion framework for controllable and realistic scenario generation. Conditioned on front-view visual observations, E2E-CDiff jointly denoises future motion states and executable low-level controls for route-interacting background vehicles. This unified state-action generation mitigates the planning-control mismatch in conventional two-stage trajectory-then-controller pipelines. Differentiable guidance further regulates speed, enforces drivable-area compliance, and supports collision-avoidance or collision-seeking behaviors, enabling both naturalistic and safety-critical scenario generation. Experiments on Bench2Drive show that E2E-CDiff achieves a favorable controllability-realism trade-off compared with representative reinforcement- and imitation-learning baselines, while its collision-guided variant induces challenging interactions across multiple autonomous driving systems. E2E-CDiff also performs competitively as a learning-based ego planner, demonstrating the generality of end-to-end state-action diffusion.

</details>


### [158] [Fabric Pneumatic Artificial Muscles Based on the Drawstring Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18641)
*Chendong Liu,Dapeng Yang,Yiming Dai,Li Jiang,Hong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于抽绳原理的织物气动人工肌肉（DPAM），相比传统气动人工肌肉在收缩时不会产生额外径向变形，具有更高的结构紧凑性、负载能力（自重的800倍以上）、最大收缩率44%、功率密度4.98 kW/kg及良好的可扩展性，并在仿生机器人和工业生产线中验证了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统气动人工肌肉在轴向收缩时会产生额外的径向变形，严重浪费可用工作空间，限制了其在紧凑空间中的应用。

Method: 受纺织品中广泛采用的抽绳原理启发，设计了一种新型织物气动人工肌肉（DPAM），通过织物结构实现收缩时无额外径向变形。

Result: DPAM展现出负载能力超过自重800倍、最大收缩率44%、功率密度高达4.98 kW/kg，并在仿生机器人和工业生产线中验证了其实用性，同时可在二维平面内轻松扩展形成矩阵。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅提供了一种高性能的新型气动人工肌肉，还启发研究人员从传统纺织结构中汲取设计灵感，以解决软体机器人领域的现有挑战。

Abstract: Pneumatic artificial muscles have wide applications in robotics and industrial fields. Conventional pneumatic artificial muscles generate extra radial deformation during axial contraction, which severely wastes available working space. Inspired by the widely adopted drawstring principle in textile products, this paper proposes a novel drawstring fabric pneumatic artificial muscle (DPAM). Unlike traditional counterparts, the proposed DPAM produces no extra radial deformation during contraction, greatly improving structural compactness. The DPAM exhibits outstanding mechanical performance: a load capacity over 800 times its self-weight, a maximum contraction ratio of 44%, and a power density up to 4.98 kW/kg, alongside excellent scalability. Two representative application scenarios, bionic robots and industrial production lines, are demonstrated to validate its practicability. The DPAM can be easily expanded within a two-dimensional plane, as verified by the fabricated DPAM matrix. This work not only presents a high-performance novel pneumatic artificial muscle but also inspires researchers to draw design inspiration from conventional textile structures to address existing challenges in soft robotics.

</details>


### [159] [MVP-Tac: A Miniaturized Dual-Modal Vision and Photoelastic Tactile Sensor for Robot-Assisted Minimally Invasive Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18660)
*Md Rakibul Islam Prince,Jaeeun Kim,Yuhao Zhou,Mason Vrshek,Shivani Reddy Sama,Adyaa Khera,Sheeraz Athar,Zijie Xu,Jiabin Liu,Shaoting Lin,Wei Li,Yu She*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MVP-Tac，一种紧凑型视觉触觉传感器，通过反射光弹性成像技术实现共定位的视觉与触觉感知，可在不牺牲视觉反馈的前提下为机器人辅助微创手术（RMIS）提供触觉反馈。实验证明其在0-2N范围内可进行力校准，在组织体模上的肿瘤触诊分类准确率达97%（暴露肿瘤）和92%（皮下肿瘤），并通过模拟结肠镜验证了双模态实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RMIS虽具优势，但缺乏触觉反馈（如触诊能力），而现有触觉方案往往无法与视觉反馈共存。实践中视觉反馈不可或缺，因此需要一种能同时提供共定位视觉与触觉感知的解决方案。

Method: 采用反射光弹性成像技术：薄层光弹性弹性体在接触时产生应力相关干涉条纹，通过微型反射偏振镜由嵌入式相机捕获；半透明膜与可控照明实现视觉模式与触觉模式的切换，实现触觉感知而不牺牲视觉。

Result: 0-2N范围内力校准验证；暴露肿瘤硬度分类准确率97%，皮下肿瘤分类准确率92%；模拟结肠镜中成功实现视觉引导的3D光映射与原位硬度分类。

Conclusion: MVP-Tac为RMIS中恢复临床实用的触诊功能提供了可行路径，同时保持必要的视觉反馈，且设计、制造和固件均已开源。

Abstract: Robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery (RMIS) offers major benefits over open and conventional laparoscopic procedures, yet it still lacks tactile feedback for palpation while operating under strict requirements to preserve reliable vision for navigation and safety. In practice, visual feedback is indispensable, and tactile solutions that cannot coexist with vision are difficult to translate into RMIS tools. To address both needs, we introduce MVP-Tac, a compact, vision-based tactile sensor that provides co-located vision and tactile sensing. MVP-Tac uses reflective photoelastic imaging: a thin photoelastic elastomer produces stress-dependent interferograms under contact that are captured by an embedded camera through a miniaturized reflective polariscope. A semi-transparent membrane and controllable illumination enable switching between visual mode and tactile mode, enabling tactile perception without sacrificing vision. We validate MVP-Tac through force calibration in the 0 to 2 N range and demonstrate its potential for tumor palpation via video-based hardness classification on tissue phantoms, achieving 97% accuracy for exposed-tumor classification and 92% accuracy for subdermal-tumor classification. Finally, we conduct a simulated colonoscopy to validate both visual and tactile modalities in a constrained lumen, including vision-guided 3D photomapping of the luminal wall and in situ hardness classification of localized nodules. Overall, MVP-Tac provides a practical path toward restoring clinically useful palpation in RMIS while maintaining essential visual feedback. The design, fabrication, and firmware of MVP-Tac are open-sourced at https://mvp-tac.github.io/

</details>


### [160] [How defensive driving enhances driving safety: A driving simulator study on drivers' defensive driving behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18663)
*Xinzheng Wu,Junyi Chen,Shaolingfeng Ye,Yong Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过驾驶模拟实验，对比高低防御性驾驶能力组的行为和眼动数据，发现具备防御性驾驶能力的驾驶员会执行更激烈的驾驶操作、更早识别风险并采取行动，从而提升驾驶安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 防御性驾驶被公认为高级驾驶技能，但其如何影响驾驶安全以及背后的机制尚缺乏充分研究。

Method: 定义了防御性驾驶的操作时机和应用场景；招募82名被试进行驾驶模拟实验，收集行为和眼动数据；根据防御性驾驶行为频率分组，进行组间和试次间比较分析。

Result: 高防御性驾驶能力组相比低能力组，加速和减速幅度更大、平均速度更低、平均绝对偏航角更大，注视时长更短、注视频率更低；高能力组更早发起防御/规避动作，降低场景风险；低能力组在试次间表现出显著差异，而高能力组大多数指标在两次试次间无显著差异。

Conclusion: 具备防御性驾驶能力的驾驶员执行更激烈的驾驶操作、更早识别和应对风险，从而提升驾驶安全；研究成果支持防御性驾驶的推广和相关培训项目，并为自动驾驶算法训练提供启示。

Abstract: Defensive driving is widely recognized as an advanced driving skill. However, whether and how defensive driving affects driving safety remains insufficiently investigated. This study examines the behavioral characteristics of defensive driving, its impact on driving safety, and the underlying mechanisms. First, defensive driving is defined regarding operational timing and application scenario. Then, 82 participants are recruited for driving simulator experiments, with their behavioral and eye movement data being collected. Following the experiments, participants are categorized into groups based on the frequency of defensive driving behaviors exhibited. Finally, both inter-group and inter trial comparisons are performed on the experimental data. Experimental results demonstrate that in the inter-group comparison, the high defensive driving capability group exhibits higher acceleration and deceleration magnitudes, lower average speeds, and larger average absolute yaw angles compared to the low capability group, alongside shorter fixation durations and reduced fixation frequencies. Moreover, we observe that these participants tend to initiate defensive or evasive actions earlier, resulting in lower scenario risk. Regarding the inter-trial comparison, we observe similar trends exclusively in the low capability group, whereas most metrics show no significant differences between Trial 1 and Trial 2 in the high capability group. These results reveal that drivers possessing defensive driving capabilities tend to execute more intense driving maneuvers and identify risks and take action earlier, thereby enhancing driving safety. Findings support the promotion of defensive driving and provide a basis for relevant training programs. Meanwhile, they offer insights for the training of autonomous driving algorithms with defensive driving capabilities.

</details>


### [161] [RoboInter1.5: A Holistic Intermediate Representation Suite for Embodied World Modeling and Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18709)
*Ziqin Wang,Hao Li,Weijun Wang,Junhao Cai,Jia Zeng,Yilun Chen,Jiangmiao Pang,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboInter1.5 是一个面向机器人操作和具身世界建模的统一中间表示套件，包含大规模密集标注数据集（23万+操作片段）、视觉问答基准、视觉-语言-动作模型以及世界模型，将中间表示作为连接低层动作空间与潜在物理仿真的双向接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人数据集存在成本高、具身特异、细粒度标注不足等问题，制约了通用推理、执行和长程环境动力学仿真能力。

Method: 构建了四部分：RoboInter-Data（571场景23万+片段，每帧标注10+种中间表示）；RoboInter-VQA（时空具身VQA任务）；RoboInter-VLA（隐式、显式、模块化规划-执行范式）；RoboInter-World（以中间表示为结构化条件信号进行可控未来状态预测）。

Result: 大量实验验证了RoboInter1.5作为统一时空脚手架的有效性，中间表示既能正则化低层动作空间，又能约束开放世界物理仿真器的潜在推演。

Conclusion: 中间表示不仅是可解释信号，更可作为双向接口，同时服务于动作执行与物理世界建模。

Abstract: Existing robot datasets remain expensive to curate, embodiment-specific, and insufficiently annotated with the fine-grained structure required for generalizable reasoning, execution, or long-horizon environment dynamics simulation. Building on our prior work, RoboInter1.0, we present RoboInter1.5, an extended and holistic suite of intermediate representations for both robotic manipulation and embodied world modeling. RoboInter1.5 provides a unified resource of data, benchmarks, and models centered on dense manipulation-oriented intermediate representations. Specifically, RoboInter-Data contains over 230k manipulation episodes across 571 scenes with dense per-frame annotations covering more than ten types of intermediate representations, including subtasks, primitive skills, object and gripper grounding, segmentation, affordance, grasp poses, contact points, motion traces, etc. Built upon these annotations, RoboInter-VQA introduces spatial and temporal embodied VQA tasks to benchmark and improve the intermediate-representation reasoning capabilities of our RoboInter-VLM. RoboInter-VLA further studies how such representations benefit action execution through implicit, explicit, and modular plan-then-execute paradigms. To better model the physical world, we further introduce RoboInter-World, which leverages intermediate representations as structured conditioning signals for controllable prediction of future world states. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that RoboInter1.5 provides a unified spatiotemporal scaffolding for intermediate representations. Rather than treating intermediate representations merely as interpretable signals, RoboInter1.5 conceptualizes them as a bidirectional interface that both regularizes low-level action spaces and constrains the latent rollouts of open-world physical simulators.

</details>


### [162] [Confidence-Gated Vision-Only Heading Alignment for UAV-UGV Cooperative Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18713)
*Reza Ahmari,Vahid Hemmati,Parham Kebria,Olusola Odeyomi,Kaushik Roy,Abdollah Homaifar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对无人机-无人车协同中基于视觉的航向预测问题，提出了一种轻量级置信门控框架，利用边界框面积和预测航向的短期波动作为可靠性代理，在低置信度时采用有界混合回退策略替代传统的冻结保持策略，实验表明该方法在执行率、准确率和平滑度之间形成了清晰权衡，并显著改善了指令级行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的航向预测并不保证所有预测结果都适合直接作为控制指令下发，因此需要研究何时以及如何信任视觉航向预测器来下发指令的决策问题。

Method: 提出轻量级置信门控框架，使用两个可解释的可靠性代理（边界框面积作为可见性代理，预测航向短期窗口变化作为稳定性代理）进行执行决策，在低置信区间对比基线冻结保持策略与有界混合回退策略。

Result: 置信门控在执行率、已执行帧准确率、下发指令准确率和平滑度之间形成了清晰权衡；稀疏执行下冻结保持策略会导致严重的过时指令误差，而有界混合回退在相同门控决策下显著改善了指令级行为。

Conclusion: 可靠的感知驱动自主性不仅取决于预测准确性，还取决于低置信度下的决策感知指令下发机制。

Abstract: Vision-based heading prediction is useful for UAV--UGV cooperation, but accurate prediction alone does not guarantee that every predicted heading should be issued directly as a control command. This paper investigates the decision problem of when and how a fixed vision-based heading predictor should be trusted for command issuance. A lightweight confidence-gated framework is proposed in which execution decisions are made using two interpretable reliability proxies derived from the perception stream: bounding-box area as a visibility-related proxy and short-window variation in predicted heading as a stability-related proxy. During low-confidence intervals, the framework compares the baseline freeze-HOLD policy with a bounded-blend fallback that updates the issued command conservatively. The method is evaluated on a real UAV--UGV dataset under clean and perturbed conditions. The results show that confidence gating creates a clear trade-off among execution rate, executed-frame accuracy, issued-command accuracy, and smoothness. The results further show that sparse execution can cause severe stale-command error under the baseline freeze-HOLD policy, whereas the bounded-blend fallback substantially improves command-level behavior under the same gate decisions. These findings highlight that reliable perception-driven autonomy depends not only on prediction accuracy, but also on decision-aware command issuance during low-confidence

</details>


### [163] [Correct-by-Construction Behavior Tree Synthesis from Signal Temporal Logic Specifications with Application to Robotic Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18731)
*Jiaheng Dong,Jingyi Huang,Liang Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从信号时序逻辑（STL）规范自动合成行为树（BT）的方法，通过区域图抽象和层次化不动点算法，生成具有严格正鲁棒性的正确性保证行为树，并在物理四旋翼平台上验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于线性时序逻辑（LTL）的正确性合成方法无法表达定量时间约束，而行为树在机器人复杂任务执行中虽广泛应用但缺乏形式化保证，因此需要一种能处理定量时序约束的正确性合成方法。

Method: 将工作空间建模为计时迁移系统并抽象为区域图，引入同时追踪逻辑进度和时序约束的增强状态空间，针对安全、可达、响应、重复和持久等STL片段设计层次化不动点算法计算获胜集，生成带有运行时约束函数的行为树子树。

Result: 仿真实验证明了规范满足具有严格正鲁棒性，物理四旋翼实验在6个STL规范下验证了实际可部署性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将信号时序逻辑的定量时间约束能力与行为树的模块化反应式控制相结合，在保证正确性的同时具备实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Behavior Trees (BTs) are widely adopted for complex task execution in robotics, providing modular, reactive control but lacking formal guarantees. However, existing correct-by-construction synthesis from Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) cannot express quantitative timing constraints. This letter synthesizes correct-by-construction BTs from Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications. The workspace is modeled as a timed transition system and abstracted into a zone graph, and an augmented state space tracking both logical progress and timing constraints is introduced. A hierarchical fixed-point algorithm computes winning sets for an STL fragment encompassing safety, reachability, response, recurrence, and persistence, yielding BT subtrees with a runtime constraint function. Correctness guarantees are proven and complexity bounds are derived. Simulations demonstrate specification satisfaction with strictly positive robustness, and a physical quadrotor experiment with six STL specifications validates practical deployability.

</details>


### [164] [Motion Primitive Discovery in a Humanoid Robot via Self-Organising Maps for Phase Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18737)
*Radovan Gregor,Igor Farkaš*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受镜像神经元系统启发，提出一种双层架构，利用自组织映射（SOM）学习手臂和手部运动拓扑表征，再通过回声状态网络（ESN）对SOM激活轨迹进行在线运动相位识别，在NICO仿人机器人上验证了该方法对七种动作相位的有效识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解动作识别的计算机制是社会认知和人机交互的核心挑战，受镜像神经元系统启发，需要一种能够在机器人平台上实现运动基元发现和在线相位识别的架构。

Method: 提出双层架构：第一层使用两个SOM分别学习手臂运动（A-SOM）和手部运动（H-SOM）的拓扑表征，通过层次相关性分析提取非冗余特征；第二层使用ESN评估SOM激活的时间轨迹（连续最佳匹配单元）是否足以实现运动相位的在线识别。

Result: 两个SOM编码了运动行为中互补的方面；基于SOM的时间轨迹保留了主导的相位判别结构；上下文信息仅提供次要的精细化作用。

Conclusion: 将SOM和ESN方法整合到MNS启发架构中是有效的，自组织运动表征在时间整合后能够支持准确的在线运动相位识别，符合相关的计算假设。

Abstract: Understanding the computational basis of action recognition is a central challenge in social cognition as well as in human-robot interaction. Inspired by the Mirror Neuron System (MNS), we propose a two-level architecture for motor primitive discovery and online phase recognition applied to the NICO humanoid robot. At the first level, two Self-Organising Maps (SOMs) learn topographic representations of arm kinematics (A-SOM) and hand kinematics (H-SOM) from simulated trials covering seven motor actions. The maps are trained on non-redundant features identified through hierarchical correlation analysis of motion trajectories. The results show that the two SOMs encode complementary aspects of motor behaviour. At the second level, an Echo State Network (ESN) evaluates whether temporal trajectories of SOM activations, represented by consecutive best-matching units, are sufficient for online recognition of the currently executed movement phase. The results show that SOM-based trajectories preserve the dominant phase-discriminative structure of the movement, while contextual information provides only a secondary refinement. Our contribution is the integration of established SOM and ESN methods within an MNS-inspired architecture for motor primitive representation and online phase recognition. The results are compatible with the computational hypothesis that self-organised motor representations, when temporally integrated, can support accurate online recognition of ongoing movement phases.

</details>


### [165] [Koopman DCM: Unstable Eigenfunctions as Data-driven Representations for Legged Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18760)
*Stéphane Caron*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将发散运动分量（DCM）推广为Koopman特征函数，通过数据驱动的方式从真实机器人数据中学习不稳定特征对，在双足机器人上实现了更好的行走模式跟踪，并与模型预测控制结合提供了基于状态的可行性约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DCM仅适用于简化模型（如线性倒立摆），缺乏通用性和数据驱动的学习框架。

Method: 将DCM重新表述为Koopman特征函数，刻意搜索具有大特征值的不稳定特征对，使用真实机器人数据训练数据驱动的可观测变量。

Result: 在一小时真实双足机器人数据上学习的DCM改善了参考行走模式的跟踪性能；与MPC结合可提供基于状态的可行性约束。

Conclusion: Koopman理论为DCM提供了更通用的形式化框架，数据驱动的DCM学习在真实机器人上有效，拓展了平衡控制的应用范围。

Abstract: In legged locomotion, divergent components of motion (DCMs) have emerged as characteristic states for balance control. They isolate the unstable mode of the dynamics but, in existing formulations, apply only to reduced models such as the linear inverted pendulum. In this study, we show how DCMs can be more generally formulated as Koopman eigenfunctions. Whereas Koopman analysis typically targets eigenvalues near zero, which capture conserved or slowly varying quantities, our investigation leads us to deliberately search for unstable eigenpairs with large eigenvalues. The resulting Koopman DCMs are data-driven observables trained using only real-robot data. On a real biped, DCMs learned from one hour of robot data improve tracking of reference walking patterns. We further show how learned DCMs provide state-based viability constraints when combined with model predictive control.

</details>


### [166] [Beyond Transformers: Linear Attention Policy for Open-Vocabulary Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18794)
*Jiahong Zhang,Yifan Lin,Yandong Zhang,Sijun Shen,Kexin Wang,Yuqi Pan,Hongjuan Pei,Wei Wang,Guoqi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出线性注意力导航（LANav），用线性注意力替代Transformer的自注意力作为策略网络，通过结构化状态更新有效提升开放词汇目标导航性能，在HM3D-OVON上达到36.4%成功率，并在真实机器人上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的策略网络在开放词汇目标导航中，性能并不随上下文窗口长度扩展，表明自注意力机制不完全适用于导航中的状态集成，因此需要探索更有效的状态更新机制。

Method: 提出LANav框架，采用线性注意力（LA）作为策略网络主干，替代传统自注意力；进一步提出加权状态扩展线性注意力（WSLA），将每个注意力头的状态扩展为多个子状态，并通过可学习的加权读出机制聚合这些子状态，实现更结构化、受调控的状态更新。

Result: LANav在HM3D-OVON上平均成功率达36.4%，宏平均成功率比Transformer基线高6.3个百分点；长距离场景提升更大；在HSSD数据集上展现鲁棒性；在宇树Go2机器人上真实部署实现82%成功率。

Conclusion: 线性注意力能有效解决Transformer自注意力在导航状态更新中的局限性，结构化状态更新机制对导航性能至关重要，LANav具有良好的计算效率与sim-to-real迁移能力。

Abstract: Open-Vocabulary Object Goal Navigation (OVON) requires agents to operate under partial observability, making effective internal state updates critical for navigation performance. This update is implemented by the policy network, where recent approaches adopt Transformer-based backbones with self-attention over a context window to integrate temporal information. However, our controlled experiments show that performance does not scale with context length under Transformer-based policies, questioning the suitability of self-attention for state integration in navigation. To this end, we propose Linear Attention-based Navigation (LANav), which adopts linear attention (LA) as the policy backbone to maintain a structured state update rather than self-attention over the context window. Across multiple LA variants evaluated under identical settings, LANav consistently outperforms Transformer-based baselines. Performance improves as state update mechanisms become more structured and regulated, highlighting the importance of state update design. To improve state update effectiveness, we introduce Weighted State-Expansion Linear Attention (WSLA), which expands each attention head's state into multiple sub-states and uses learnable weighted readout to aggregate expanded sub-states. Equipped with WSLA, LANav achieves 36.4% average success rate (SR) on HM3D-OVON, outperforming Transformer-based counterparts by 6.3 percentage points in macro-averaged SR, while maintaining computational efficiency. Distance-stratified results show larger gains in long-distance episodes, while HSSD transfer and fine-tuning demonstrate robustness across scene distributions. Real-world deployment on a Unitree Go2 further achieves an 82% success rate over 50 trials, supporting the practical feasibility and sim-to-real transfer of LANav.

</details>


### [167] [WorldScape Policy 2.0: Empowering Steerable World Action Modeling with Reasoning-Augmented Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18840)
*Haisheng Su,Zongdai Liu,Xin Jin,Haoxuan Dou,Chengming Hu,Baorun Li,Zhanwang Liu,Ruiyan Xu,Jianjie Fang,Xin Zhang,Zhenjie Yang,Xue Yang,Chen Gao,Junchi Yan,Yong Li,Wei Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WorldScape Policy 2.0，一种融合推理增强型长短期记忆的可控世界动作模型，通过短期视觉记忆、长短期事件记忆和ManipEvent-5M数据集实现细粒度多模态可控性，在长程自主规划、细粒度指令跟随和上下文适应方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型受限于有限的时间上下文、粗粒度的episode级语言监督以及以文本为主的条件控制，导致难以跟踪任务进度、实现细粒度语言-视频-动作对齐，并限制了视觉上下文推理和跨本体迁移能力。

Method: 提出WorldScape Policy 2.0，包含：(1) 因果短期视觉记忆，将近期观测作为DiT预填充以保留局部交互动态；(2) 长短期事件记忆，将历史VLM输出组织为全局历史、局部活跃和事件边界三种表示，实现进度感知检索；(3) 检索历史增强感知和自回归生成的规划token，形成隐式子目标条件；(4) 语义强制将事件级指令语义注入潜在规划路径；(5) 构建ManipEvent-5M数据集，含近500万个事件片段，配对齐的动作轨迹、任务指令、子任务描述、目标图像和视频演示。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人平台上的实验表明，该方法在长程自主规划、细粒度指令跟随和上下文适应方面具有优越性能。

Conclusion: 这些设计为从高层指令进行自主规划和从细粒度文本、目标图像或视频上下文提示进行可控执行提供了统一接口，有效解决了现有WAM的局限性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) offer a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation by jointly modeling visual state transitions and robot actions. However, existing WAMs are constrained by limited temporal context, coarse episode-level language supervision, and predominantly text-only conditioning, which hinder task-progress tracking and fine-grained language-video-action grounding while limiting visual-context reasoning and cross-embodiment transfer. In this paper, we introduce WorldScape Policy 2.0, a controllable WAM with reasoning-augmented long short-term memory. Its causal short-term visual memory supplies recent observations as DiT prefill to preserve local interaction dynamics, while its long short-term event memory organizes historical VLM outputs into global-history, local-active, and event-boundary representations for progress-aware retrieval. The retrieved history augments perception and autoregressively generated planning tokens, yielding an implicit subgoal condition for autonomous planning; semantic forcing further transfers event-level instruction semantics into this latent planning pathway. To establish fine-grained multimodal controllability, we construct ManipEvent-5M, an event-grounded embodied pretraining dataset containing nearly 5 million event segments with aligned action trajectories, episode-level task instructions, segment-level subtask captions, goal images, and video demonstrations. These designs provide a unified interface for autonomous planning from high-level instructions and controllable execution from fine-grained text, goal-image, or video-context prompts. Experiments in both simulation and real-world platforms demonstrate superior capabilities in long-horizon autonomous planning, fine-grained instruction following and in-context adaptation.

</details>


### [168] [Pose-Parameterized Motion Planning and CBF-QP Self-Collision Filtering for a Long-Reach Drilling Boom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18855)
*Mehdi Heydari Shahna,Tuomo Kivelä,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究针对Sandvik SB60长臂钻机，将姿态参数化规划与基于胶囊体的控制屏障函数二次规划（CBF-QP）相结合，在测量状态逆运动学（IK）中实现了无自碰撞的运动规划。离线绕路规划器通过23个杆段-身体区域距离筛选候选路径点，在线CBF-QP利用14个胶囊对约束过滤关节速度。在Simscape多体模型中验证，该方法在数十万样本中零IK失败，绕路路径点生成成功，最终位置误差达毫米级，CBF裕度均高于设定阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长臂钻机在操作员监督控制向自主控制过渡时，需要实现防自碰撞的运动规划与执行，现有方法缺乏针对此类重型机械的有效碰撞感知方案。

Method: 将姿态参数化规划与胶囊体控制屏障函数二次规划（CBF-QP）结合到测量状态逆运动学（IK）中。离线阶段使用23个杆段-身体区域距离筛选候选路径点，在线阶段使用14个胶囊对约束通过九基元全身胶囊模型过滤关节速度。

Result: 在两个钻探任务（五目标受限姿态巡回和三目标全姿态巡回）中，数十万采样下零IK失败，成功生成绕路路径点，最终位置误差达毫米级，CBF裕度均高于报告阈值。

Conclusion: 该方法首次在工业级钻机仿真中验证了结合参数化规划与CBF-QP的碰撞感知运动规划策略，实现了无需目标特定重新调参的链式运动。

Abstract: Long-reach drilling booms must reach successive poses without self-collision. Moving from operator-supervised control toward autonomy requires collision-aware motion planning and execution. For the Sandvik SB60, this study adapts established methods by integrating pose-parameterized planning with a capsule-based control barrier function quadratic program (CBF-QP) in measured-state inverse kinematics (IK). A fixed task-specific parameter set within each task generates waypoints, detours, timed references, and chained motion without target-specific retuning. The offline detour planner screens candidate waypoints using 23 selected rod-segment-to-body-region distances, whereas the online CBF-QP filters joint velocities using 14 configured capsule-pair constraints from a nine-primitive whole-body capsule model. Evaluation considers two drilling tasks in a manufacturer-developed SB60 Simscape Multibody model: a five-target restricted-orientation tour and a three-target full-pose tour. Across several hundred thousand samples, the method produced zero IK failures, generated several detour waypoints, achieved millimetre-level mean final-position error, and recorded no sampled CBF margins below the reported thresholds.

</details>


### [169] [NGPS: GPS-Denied Aerial Geo-Localization and 2.5D Reconstruction via Deep Satellite Image Matching and Multi-Rate Sensor Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18936)
*Sanket Sharma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NGPS是一种面向高空无人机的新型视觉地理定位系统，通过将下视图像与卫星影像匹配实现GPS-free绝对定位。系统融合自适应置信加权UKF、速度预测核提取和非同步多速率时序优先级队列，在60-150米飞行高度上实现2.94米位置RMSE，相比单目VIO提升3.5倍，并在Jetson Orin NX上实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高空无人机在GPS信号缺失或受干扰环境下的绝对定位问题，同时克服纯视觉惯性里程计(VIO)存在的累积漂移，实现高精度、鲁棒的全局定位。

Method: (1) 自适应置信加权UKF融合：利用RANSAC内点率、重投影误差和匹配置信度动态调制NGPS协方差；(2) 速度预测核提取：利用VIO速度预测卫星图像搜索区域；(3) 异步多速率时序优先级队列：按时间顺序交错融合绝对定位(1-2Hz)、VIO(10-20Hz)和IMU(100-200Hz)更新。全局优化采用VINS位姿图优化，以NGPS校正为锚点。

Result: 在5条飞行序列(60-150米AGL)上，NGPS位置RMSE为2.94米，最差ATE为6.04米(150米AGL、2m/s)，相比单目VIO提升3.5倍。系统在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX上实时运行。

Conclusion: NGPS有效实现了高空无人机GPS-free视觉地理定位，显著优于纯VIO方法，具备实时性和实用性，部分实现已开源。

Abstract: We present NGPS (Next-Generation Positioning System), a visual geo-localization framework for high-altitude UAVs that provides GPS-free absolute positioning by matching down-facing images to georeferenced satellite imagery with deep features. The system combines (1) adaptive confidence-weighted UKF fusion, where NGPS covariance is modulated by RANSAC inlier ratio, reprojection error, and match confidence; (2) velocity-predictive kernel extraction, using VIO velocity to predict the satellite search region; and (3) an asynchronous multi-rate temporal priority queue that interleaves absolute position (1-2 Hz), VIO (10-20 Hz), and IMU (100-200 Hz) updates in chronological order. Globally optimized poses from VINS pose-graph optimization, anchored by NGPS corrections, further enable real-time 2.5D georeferenced orthomosaic reconstruction. On five flight sequences (60-150 m AGL), NGPS achieves 2.94 m position RMSE, with worst-case ATE 6.04 m at 150 m AGL and 2 m/s, yielding a 3.5x improvement over standalone monocular VIO. The system runs in real time on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX. Part of the implementation is open-sourced at https://github.com/snktshrma/ngps_flight.

</details>


### [170] [The Twist Decomposition of Serial Robots Under Lower-Mobility Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18940)
*Luc Baron,Damien Chablat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于几何扭量投影器的串行机构末端扭量分解框架，用于低自由度任务，直接区分任务相关与冗余运动，并实现紧凑的逆运动学求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖雅可比零空间投影来区分任务相关与冗余运动，不够直观且缺乏几何直接性，需要一种更自然、更直观的扭量分解方式。

Method: 引入几何定义的扭量投影器，将末端执行器的扭量分解为任务分量和冗余分量，替代传统的雅可比零空间投影方法。

Result: 实现了任务相关运动与任务无关运动在操作空间中的直接和直观区分，并由此导出了紧凑的逆运动学求解方案。

Conclusion: 该框架能够自然地处理操作臂冗余和任务冗余，为低自由度任务的串行操作臂提供了一种更简洁、几何意义明确的运动学分解途径。

Abstract: This paper introduces a twist decomposition framework for serial manipulators performing lower mobility tasks. Rather than relying on Jacobian null-space projections, the method separates the end-effector twist into task and redundant components using geometrically defined twist projectors. This formulation provides a direct and intuitive distinction between task-relevant and task-irrelevant motions in operational space, enabling a compact inverse kinematics scheme that naturally handles both manipulator and task redundancy.

</details>


### [171] [Design and stability analysis of an underactuated hand with passively rotating fingers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18950)
*Léonie Plancoulaine,Sylvain Guégan,Franck Plestan,Damien Chablat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种具有空间移动能力的欠驱动机械手指的创新设计与稳定性分析，通过基座旋转关节和差动机构实现圆柱、球形、平行和包络抓取，实验验证了其在降低机械复杂度、成本和控制需求的同时保持功能适应性，适用于农业、物流、辅助技术和废物分拣等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的机械手设计复杂、成本高、控制难度大，尤其在非结构化环境中难以同时兼顾灵巧性和鲁棒性。作者旨在通过欠驱动和被动适应性设计，简化机械结构、降低控制复杂度，同时保持对不同形状物体的可靠抓取能力。

Method: 每根手指仅含两个指节，基座采用转动关节实现被动空间旋转；引入差动机构将单一致动扭矩分配至多根手指，实现自适应协调运动；基于接触力在手指基关节处产生的力矩建立稳定性判据，确保抓取可靠性；通过全机械样机实验验证理论分析。

Result: 实验验证了所提出的手指结构能够实现圆柱形、球形、平行和包络抓取等多种抓取模式；欠驱动设计有效降低了机械复杂度、成本和控制需求；稳定性判据能够可靠评估抓取过程中物体被弹出的风险。

Conclusion: 该欠驱动空间手指设计在简化机械和控制复杂度的同时，展现出良好的抓取适应性和稳定性，为开发适用于非结构化环境的灵巧且鲁棒的机械手提供了可行方案。未来工作将聚焦于自动化驱动和精细控制策略优化。

Abstract: This paper presents an innovative design and stability analysis of an underactuated robotic finger with spatial mobility, designed to enhance gripping dexterity in robotic hands. The finger architecture incorporates a revolute joint at its base, enabling passive spatial rotation that facilitates both cylindrical and spherical grasping. With only two phalanges per finger, the design simplifies kinematic complexity while supporting precision and enveloping grasps. Stability criteria, based on the moment at the finger base joint induced by contact forces, are introduced to ensure reliable object gripping and prevent ejection during manipulation. The study also examines a differential mechanism that distributes a single actuation torque across multiple fingers, allowing adaptive and coordinated motion. This mechanism enhances the hand's ability to grasp diverse object shapes with minimal pre-grasp adjustments, leveraging passivity for autonomous adaptation. Theoretical findings are experimentally validated using a fully mechanical prototype, demonstrating versatility in performing cylindrical, spherical, parallel, and enveloping grasps. The integration of underactuation-both within individual fingers and among multiple fingers-reduces mechanical complexity, cost, and control demands while preserving functional adaptability. This work advances the development of compliant robotic hands suitable for applications requiring dexterity and robustness, such as agricultural robotics, logistics, assistive technologies, and waste sorting. Future research will focus on automating actuation and refining control strategies to further improve grasp stability and precision, paving the way for autonomous manipulation in unstructured environments.

</details>


### [172] [Bayesian Retraction Optimization for Tissue Attachment Mapping in Surgical Dissection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19174)
*Shing-Hei Ho,Bao Thach,Toan Vo,James M. Ferguson,Alan Kuntz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于贝叶斯序贯希尔伯特图（SBHM）的机器人组织解剖方法，通过概率建模识别组织附着点，并结合贝叶斯牵拉优化（BRO）选择最优动作，在仿真和真实机器人实验中实现零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外科医生短缺问题日益严重，自动化手术子任务（如组织解剖）有助于减轻工作负担并扩大患者诊疗可及性。现有方法或依赖手工制定的切割策略（无法量化不确定性），或依赖仿真方法（需要强建模假设），缺乏对组织附着识别这一固有概率问题的有效处理。

Method: 1) 采用序贯贝叶斯希尔伯特图（SBHM）表示每个组织点附着于下方切除表面的概率；2) 使用集成学习分类器作为噪声信息源，从机器人组织牵拉过程中获取的空间数据预测附着似然；3) 提出贝叶斯牵拉优化（BRO），在安全约束下选择信息量最大的牵拉动作；4) SBHM随推理更新，高附着似然区域被选择性切割。

Result: 在多种组织几何形状和采集策略的仿真环境中验证了方法有效性，并在真实机器人解剖实验中实现了零样本迁移。

Conclusion: 该方法避免了显式组织建模，通过概率方式处理附着识别问题，在仿真和真实场景中均表现出良好的泛化能力，为自动化手术提供了可行方案。

Abstract: With growing surgeon shortages, automating surgical sub-tasks such as tissue dissection offers a promising step toward reducing workload and expanding patient access. Prior work has relied on hand-crafted incision policies that cannot quantify uncertainty or has relied on simulation-based methods that require strong modeling assumptions. We instead view tissue attachment identification as an inherently probabilistic problem and propose a Bayesian approach that avoids explicit tissue modeling. Our method uses a Sequential Bayesian Hilbert Map (SBHM) to represent the likelihood that each tissue point is attached to the underlying resection surface. An ensemble of learned classifiers predicts attachment likelihoods from spatial data acquired during robotic tissue retraction, with each classifier serving as a noisy information source to update the SBHM. To plan the next retraction, we devise Bayesian Retraction Optimization (BRO) to select the most informative action under safety constraints. As the SBHM refines over time, regions with high attachment likelihood are selectively incised. We validate our method in simulation across diverse tissue geometries and acquisition strategies, and demonstrate zero-shot transfer to real robotic dissection experiments.

</details>


### [173] [Agentic Real2Sim: Physics-based World Modeling with Vision-Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19190)
*Guanxiong Chen,Qianjun Xia,Jiawei Peng,Heng Zhang,Bole Ma,Justin Qian,Ziyi Jiao,Bingyang Zhou,Luoxin Ye,Kaifeng Zhang,Kunyi Wang,Weijia Zeng,Yunuo Chen,Pengzhi Yang,Ziqiu Zeng,Huamin Wang,Chao Liu,Alan Yuille,Fan Shi,Changxi Zheng,Yunzhu Li,Chenfanfu Jiang,Peter Yichen Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Real2Sim框架，利用视觉-语言智能体将真实世界的物体-机器人交互记录自动转换为可运行的物理仿真场景（即“孪生体”），在刚性物体操作、可变形物体交互和人形运动场景中验证了效果，并使用开放权重的VLM后端以较低成本实现与前沿模型相当的转换成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的真实到仿真（Real2Sim）转换过程高度依赖人工：需要手动调整视觉基础模型、清理网格、对齐坐标框架，并在视觉感知工具和仿真器之间编写脆弱的胶水代码，流程繁琐且费力。

Method: 提出Agentic Real2Sim框架，采用视觉-语言智能体（VLM后端驱动）自动执行场景几何恢复、物体状态推断、物理参数估计，并将演员、物体、相机、位姿和轨迹组装成可运行的物理仿真环境。

Result: 在刚性物体操作、可变形物体交互和人形运动等不同领域场景中验证了效果；使用开放权重VLM后端可以以远低于前沿模型的成本获得相当的转换成功率。

Conclusion: 该工作向可扩展的Real2Sim转换迈出了第一步，生成的与真实世界对齐的孪生体可用于下游机器人任务（如策略学习与评估）。

Abstract: Real-to-sim conversion for robotic interaction with objects remains labor-intensive because it requires more than visual reconstruction: a streamlined real2sim process must recover scene geometries and object states, infer physical parameters, and assemble actors, objects, cameras, poses, and trajectories into a runnable physical simulation. Today this process still depends on manual tuning of visual foundation models, mesh cleanup, coordinate-frame alignment, and brittle workflow glue across visual perception tools and simulators. We introduce \textit{Agentic Real2Sim}, a framework for generalized physical world modeling with vision-language agents, converting a real-world recording of object-robot interaction into a simulatable episodic twin which preserves observations, geometries, robot interactions, and object states. We evaluate Agentic Real2Sim on rigid-object manipulation, deformable-object interaction, and humanoid motion scenes, spanning domains that are usually handled by separate Real2Sim pipelines, marking a first step toward scalable conversion. The framework's agentic decisions can be driven by an open-weight VLM backend at a small fraction of the cost of frontier models, while attaining comparable conversion success rate. We aim to use the resulting real-world-aligned twins for downstream robotics tasks, specifically policy learning and evaluation. The project site is available at https://ericchen321.github.io/agentic_real2sim.github.io/.

</details>


### [174] [Cognitive Dual-Process Planning for Autonomous Driving with Structured Scene Knowledge and Verifiable Reasoning-Action Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19194)
*Zhongyao Yang,Haoyu Li,Yu Yan,Zhuangxuan Yu,Jiangfeng Nan,Jinrui Nan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于认知双过程（快慢思考）的自动驾驶规划框架，利用结构化思维链（S-CoT）、场景复杂度估计器（Arbiter）以及规则验证器，在无需人工标注的情况下生成可验证的规划推理，实现高准确率与低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs在自动驾驶规划中虽有显式推理优势，但面临结构化监督成本高、常规场景推理冗余、推理与动作不一致等问题，亟需一种高效、自洽且可验证的规划框架。

Method: 构建双过程规划框架：（1）自动化数据引擎结合感知基础模型、关键路径过滤和专家VLM生成结构化思维链（S-CoT）；（2）轻量视觉Arbiter通过多级视觉编码特征评估场景复杂度，路由至快速元动作预测（快路径）或结构化推理（慢路径）；（3）慢路径输出经确定性规则验证器检查S-CoT字段与最终动作一致性，提供可验证奖励用于GRPO优化。

Result: 在195场景手动审计中，生成标注的CoT准确率达91.8%，逻辑一致性得分（LCS）98.5%；在574个NAVSIM测试样本上，规划准确率80.14%，LCS 97.20%，相比全慢推理场景平均延迟降低17.39%。

Conclusion: 该框架通过自适应推理与规则验证，成功将显式场景知识落用于高层VLM规划决策中，在保证高逻辑一致性的同时降低了推理延迟，并识别了路由与规划性能下降的边界条件。

Abstract: High-level planning for autonomous driving is a knowledge-intensive engineering decision task that requires accurate scene understanding, timely inference, and internally consistent action selection. Vision-language models (VLMs) can make intermediate reasoning explicit, but their use in deployed planners is constrained by costly structured supervision, unnecessary reasoning in routine scenes, and possible inconsistencies between generated rationales and driving actions. We present a cognitive dual-process planning framework that represents planning-relevant scene knowledge in a machine-parsable structured chain-of-thought (S-CoT) schema. An automated data engine integrates perception foundation models, critical-path filtering, and an expert VLM to generate S-CoT supervision without manual annotation of individual rationales. A lightweight visual Arbiter estimates scene complexity from multilevel vision-encoder features before language decoding and routes each input to either fast meta-action prediction or slow structured reasoning. For slow-path outputs, a deterministic rule-based validator checks whether the parsed S-CoT fields are consistent with the final meta-action and provides verifiable rewards for Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). In a 195-scene manual audit, the generated annotations achieve 91.8\% CoT accuracy and a 98.5\% Logical Consistency Score (LCS). On 574 manually verified NAVSIM test samples, the planner achieves 80.14\% planning accuracy and 97.20\% LCS while reducing average latency by 17.39\% relative to applying slow reasoning to every scene. Evaluation on external long-tail subsets further identifies conditions under which routing and planning performance degrade. Together, these results show how explicit scene knowledge can be operationalized through adaptive reasoning and rule-based verification to support high-level VLM planning decisions.

</details>


### [175] [Computing on the Fly: Navigating a Vision for the Future of Drone Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19213)
*Kevin Butler,Christopher Stewart,Nils Aschenbruck,Alina Gerall,Weisong Shi,Deborah Silver,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 报告展望了未来十年无人机将像高速公路和电网一样成为国家基础设施，用于运送货物、医疗物资和信息，但当前存在"能力差距"——硬件和愿景超前于安全大规模运营所需的软件和系统。报告识别了12项关键技术挑战，涵盖规模化、AI智能、边缘计算、自主系统、数据基础设施、关键设施保护、可信赖性、安全认证、人机协作、标准监管及人才培养等方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动无人机技术从当前有限应用扩展到国家基础设施级别的规模化运营，实现灾害检测、医疗运输、基础设施巡检等变革性应用。

Method: 识别并系统阐述12项关键挑战及对应解决方案，构建多维度发展路线图。

Result: 提出12项技术挑战清单：规模化部署、AI智能与保障、边云协同、自主智能体、数据训练基础设施、关键设施保护、非确定性智能体构建可靠机群、信任与安全认证、下一代网络、人机协作、标准认证监管、劳动力教育。

Conclusion: 要弥合硬件愿景与软件系统之间的"能力差距"，需同步推进上述12个技术方向的突破，才能实现无人机的大规模安全运营和基础设施级应用。

Abstract: The report envisions a decade in which drones move goods, medical supplies, and information at a scale comparable to national infrastructure investments like highways and the electric grid. Potential applications include natural disaster detection drones that spot wildfire sources within minutes, medical supply chains that bypass ground congestion to reach rural hospitals, and nationwide fleets that continuously inspect bridges and power lines. Realizing this future, however, requires closing what report authors call a "capability gap," where hardware and aspirations are outpacing the software and systems needed to operate safely at scale.
  The report identifies twelve technical challenges that must be addressed to realize the transformative potential of drone technology: Scaling to millions of drones; AI intelligence and assurance; Edge-cloud continuum and real-time coordination; AI autonomy and agentic systems; Data, training, and validation infrastructure; Critical infrastructure protection; Building reliable fleets from non-deterministic agents; Trust, security, and distributed authentication; Next-generation drone networks; Human-AI partnership and scalable insight; Standards, certification, and regulation; and Workforce development and education.
  These twelve challenges and proposed approaches to them form the basis of the report, laying out a multifaceted path forward for the evolution of done technology.

</details>


### [176] [Eversion-based robots can enable safe access,steering and endoscopic imaging within the spinal subarachnoid space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19274)
*Zicong Wu,Panagiotis Kalozoumis,S. M. Hadi Sadati,Aminul I. Ahmed,Jonathan Shapey,Christian Baker,Thomas Booth,Wenfeng Xia,Sebastien Ourselin,Panagiotis Vartholomeos,Christos Bergeles*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种直径2mm的翻转生长机器人平台，通过压力驱动的尖端翻转实现人脊髓蛛网膜下腔内的低摩擦导航与转向，经计算建模、体模实验和完整人体尸体研究验证，相比传统推入式导管，平均交互力降低65.2%，峰值力降低48.0%，并在尸体中实现150mm可控鞘内延伸且未造成可见神经损伤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脊髓蛛网膜下腔解剖结构狭窄、柔顺且脆弱，传统导管和连续体机器人依赖近端推送，会产生摩擦和剪切力，限制远端可控性并增加神经损伤风险，亟需一种能最小化摩擦和剪切力的新型导航平台。

Method: 开发直径2mm的翻转生长机器人，集成微型内窥镜，通过压力驱动尖端翻转变形实现前进，将运动定位于远端尖端，最小化已展开体部的平移滑动；结合计算建模、体模实验和完整人体尸体研究进行多模态验证。

Result: 体模实验中，平均交互力降低65.2%，峰值交互力降低48.0%；计算建模显示翻转生长重新分布组织载荷，降低局部应力集中和界面剪切；完整人体尸体中实现150mm可控鞘内延伸，经荧光镜和内窥镜可视化确认，术后椎板切除和硬膜切开未发现硬膜或神经结构的宏观损伤。

Conclusion: 该研究首次在完整人体脊柱解剖中实现了基于翻转生长的机器人导航的力学表征和多模态验证，为未来鞘内介入治疗建立了定量和程序性基础，但仍需在更大解剖队列和生理条件下进一步验证才能进入临床转化。

Abstract: Safe navigation within the spinal subarachnoid space is constrained by its narrow, compliant, and delicate anatomy. Conventional catheters and continuum robots rely on proximal pushing, generating friction and shear along the tissue device interface that limit distal controllability and increase the risk of neural injury.
