Here, we present a 2 mm diameter eversion-growing robotic platform that enables friction minimised extension and steering within the human spinal subarachnoid space, validated through computational modelling, phantom experiments, and intact human cadaver studies. The robot integrates a miniature endoscope for real time intrathecal visualisation and advances by pressure driven tip eversion, localising motion to the distal tip while minimising translational sliding of the deployed body. Phantom experiments demonstrated reductions of 65.2% in mean interaction force and 48.0% in peak interaction force compared with matched push-based insertion. Physics based modelling showed that eversion based growth redistributed tissue loading, reducing local stress concentrations and interfacial shear relative to conventional insertion. In an intact human cadaver, the system achieved 150 mm of controlled intrathecal extension with concurrent fluoroscopic and endoscopic visualisation, providing access across multiple vertebral levels from a standard lumbar entry point. Postprocedural laminectomy and durotomy revealed no observable macroscopic disruption of the dura mater or surrounding neural structures. These results provide the first mechanically characterised and multimodally validated demonstration of eversion-based robotic navigation in intact human spinal anatomy, establishing a quantitative and procedural foundation for future intrathecal interventions. Further validation in larger anatomical cohorts and under physiological conditions will be required before clinical translation.

</details>


### [177] [Stochastic Multi-Objective Kinodynamic Planning Against Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19284)
*Thomas Marshall Vielmetti,Daniel Cherenson,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SMO-RRT和SMO-SST两种算法，用于在存在随机混合对手的环境中解决多目标运动学规划问题，通过在闭环策略序列空间中进行规划并集成基于蒙特卡洛粒子展开的样本风险评估，构建帕累托前沿路径，并证明了概率完备性和机会约束 violation 的有限样本界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机会约束规划器在开环轨迹上评估风险，产生过于保守的解，未能考虑自我智能体的反应能力。本文旨在解决这一局限，通过将规划空间转移到闭环策略序列，并在树构建过程中直接集成基于样本的风险评估。

Method: 1) 将规划空间从开环轨迹转移到闭环策略序列；2) 通过蒙特卡洛粒子展开将样本风险评估直接集成到树构建中；3) 提出SMO-RRT（证明概率完备性）和SMO-SST（通过选择性剪枝提升数值性能）两种算法；4) 推导了非高斯、状态依赖不确定性系统下机会约束 violation 概率的有限样本界。

Result: SMO-RRT被证明具有概率完备性；SMO-SST通过选择性剪枝提升了数值性能但牺牲了完备性；两种算法均导出了适用于非高斯、状态依赖不确定性系统的机会约束 violation 概率有限样本界。

Conclusion: 该方法使得在广泛环境中实现概率安全规划成为可能，适用于多智能体系统、社交导航和自动驾驶等领域。

Abstract: This paper addresses multi-objective kinodynamic planning in environments with stochastic hybrid adversaries that probabilistically transition to adversarial modes based on the ego state. The goal is to construct the Pareto-front of paths that trade off execution cost and the probability of safety constraint violation (risk). Existing chance-constrained planners evaluate risk over open-loop trajectories, yielding overly conservative solutions that fail to account for ego-agent reactivity. To address this limitation, we shift the planning space to sequences of closed-loop policies, and integrate sample-based risk evaluation directly into tree construction via Monte-Carlo particle rollouts. We first introduce Stochastic Multi-Objective RRT (SMO-RRT), for which we prove probabilistic completeness, followed by Stochastic Multi-Objective Stable Sparse RRT (SMO-SST), which leverages selective pruning to improve numerical performance at the cost of completeness. For both algorithms, we derive a finite-sample bound on the probability of chance constraint violation for systems with non-Gaussian, state-dependent uncertainty, enabling probabilistically safe planning in a broad class of environments applicable to multi-agent systems, social navigation, and autonomous driving.

</details>


### [178] [From Distances to Trajectories: Real-Time Signed Distance Function Mapping and Distance-Accelerated Motion Planning for UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19306)
*Jason Stanley,Zhirui Dai,Qihao Qian,Tzu-Chin Ho,Tianxing Fan,Siddharth Saha,Christopher Barngrover,Ki Myung Brian Lee,Nikolay Atanasov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协同设计的映射与规划框架，使用符号距离函数（SDF）作为统一表征。通过Octree残差网络（OREN）在线重建SDF，结合Bubble*搜索规划器利用距离信息构建最大无碰撞球体（bubbles），实现无人机在杂乱环境中的实时自主飞行，相比基线方法SDF估计提升22%，规划时间从10秒降至1-3秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将映射与规划分离处理，依赖二元占据栅格进行碰撞检测，忽略了更丰富的距离信息，导致效率低下。本文认为映射与规划应围绕SDF这一统一表征进行协同设计，以同时获得更优的映射精度和规划效率。

Method: 提出Octree残差网络（OREN），将显式八叉树先验与隐式神经残差相结合，兼具体素方法的高效性和神经网络方法的精确性与可微性；提出Bubble*搜索规划器，利用SDF的距离信息在自由空间中生长最大无碰撞球体（bubbles），构建bubble图进行搜索，提供终止、完备性和故障检测的正式保证。

Result: OREN在SDF估计上相比基线提升22%；Bubble*在杂乱环境中规划约90米轨迹仅需1-3秒，而基线方法需10秒。在四旋翼无人机上完成了实时在线验证。

Conclusion: SDF作为统一表征能够有效桥接映射与规划两大任务，OREN-Bubble*集成方案在计算受限的无人机平台上实现了实时自主导航，验证了协同设计的有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous flight in cluttered environments requires a robot to build a geometric map of its surroundings and plan safe, dynamically feasible trajectories, all onboard and in real time. Conventional approaches treat mapping and planning as separate stages and often rely on binary occupancy for collision checking. We argue that these two stages should be co-designed around a single representation: a signed distance function (SDF). By encoding distance to the nearest obstacle, an SDF provides richer information for planning and trajectory optimization than occupancy alone. We develop an Octree REsidual Network (OREN) that pairs an explicit octree prior with an implicit neural residual to reconstruct SDFs online from point cloud observations with the efficiency of volumetric methods and the accuracy and differentiability of neural methods. In tandem, we develop Bubble$^\star$, a search-based planner that exploits the distance information to grow maximal collision-free balls, which we call bubbles, with formal guarantees of termination, completeness, and failure detection. Planning over a graph of bubbles significantly reduces collision checks compared to a grid-based A$^\star$ search and returns a bubble sequence that forms a safe corridor for trajectory optimization. We demonstrate the integrated OREN-Bubble$^\star$ approach onboard a quadrotor, navigating unseen indoor environments in real time under tight compute constraints. OREN improves SDF estimation by $22$% compared to baselines, while Bubble$^\star$ finds trajectories spanning $\approx 90$ m through a cluttered environment in $1$-$3$ sec., whereas baselines take up to $10$ sec. in the same environment.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [FSDBN: Foreground-Aware EEG--Visual Alignment via Dynamic Brain Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18344)
*Yiheng Liu,Chuhang Zheng,Peiliang Gong,Jingtao Liu,Daoqiang Zhang,Qi Zhu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出FSDBN框架，通过语义一致性显著性对齐和动态门控融合解决EEG视觉解码中的前景-背景感知不对称问题，在零样本脑到图像检索任务上达到69.0% top-1和92.2% top-5准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG视觉解码方法忽略了复杂场景中前景与背景的感知不对称性，导致背景干扰和语义对齐错误；同时EEG信号快速时间动态性和非平稳空间模式使得捕捉与视觉注意力相关的时变脑连接变得困难。

Method: 提出FSDBN统一框架，包含：(1) 语义一致性显著性对齐，在联合显著性和语义约束下将语义相关的前景区域与背景噪声分离；(2) 语义先验动态门控前景融合，自适应调节前景和背景特征的贡献；(3) 将EEG信号建模为自适应时空脑网络，其功能连接动态重组以捕捉对显著前景的神经响应。

Result: 在零样本脑到图像检索任务上，FSDBN达到69.0% top-1准确率和92.2% top-5准确率，超越此前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模前景-背景感知不对称性并动态建模EEG时变脑网络，FSDBN有效提升了EEG视觉解码的鲁棒性和语义对齐性能。

Abstract: EEG-based visual decoding provides a non-invasive pathway for interpreting visual semantics. However, existing methods often overlook the perceptual asymmetry between foreground and background in complex scenes, leading to background interference and semantic misalignment. EEG signals also exhibit rapid temporal dynamics and nonstationary spatial patterns, making it difficult to capture the time-varying brain connectivity associated with focal visual attention. To address these limitations, we propose FSDBN, a unified framework for robust EEG-visual decoding. FSDBN introduces Semantic-Consistent Saliency Alignment to separate semantically relevant foreground regions from background noise under joint saliency and semantic constraints. It further employs Semantic-Prior Dynamic Gating Foreground Fusion to adaptively regulate the contributions of foreground and background features. In parallel, EEG signals are modeled as adaptive spatiotemporal brain networks whose functional connectivity dynamically reorganizes to capture neural responses to salient foregrounds. Experiments on zero-shot brain-to-image retrieval demonstrate that FSDBN achieves 69.0 percent top-1 accuracy and 92.2 percent top-5 accuracy, outperforming previous state-of-the-art methods. Code is available at https://github.com/LiuYiheng1/FSDBN-EEG.

</details>


### [180] [Thinking Fast, Thinking Slow: Adaptive Multimodal Transformer-based Sensor Fusion for Depth Estimation on Ultra-low-power MCUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18897)
*Luca Crupi,Lorenzo Lamberti,Giovanni Badaracco,Daniele Allegri,Alessandro Giusti,Daniele Palossi*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应多模态传感器融合方法，结合轻量级循环Transformer架构（68.8万参数）和基于置信度的门控机制，在超低功耗嵌入式平台上实现深度图估计。通过迭代间标记传播和增量传感器使用策略，在NYUv2数据集上以仅损失4.8%的δ1精度换取90%的能耗节省（2.44mJ/帧），并在GAP9 SoC上完成原型验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超低功耗（<100mW）嵌入式平台上，增加传感器数量虽能提升可靠性和鲁棒性，但导致能耗与精度之间存在严重权衡。现有方法难以在极端资源约束下实现自适应传感器调度，亟需一种能够动态平衡能耗与预测精度的轻量级AI融合方案。

Method: 1) 多模态传感器融合：相机、超声波和ToF传感器；2) 轻量级循环Transformer架构（688k参数），通过标记传播保持时间一致性；3) 增量传感器利用机制：每次迭代根据置信度门控信号决定是否引入更高功耗的传感器；4) 基于GWT GAP9多核SoC的定制PCB硬件原型。

Result: 1) 相比全传感器全迭代基线，仅损失4.8%的δ1精度，节省90%能耗（2.44 mJ/帧）；2) 相比MobileDepth，参数减少9倍，δ1精度仅低5.6%；3) 相比GAP9上的SOTA模型，在相同平均功耗（~400mW）下δ1精度提升31.8%。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应门控机制与增量传感器融合策略在超低功耗平台上实现了精度-能耗的帕累托最优，验证了通过动态计算路径调度在极端资源约束下部署复杂多模态AI任务的可行性。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI)-based multimodal sensor fusion is a relevant topic gaining ever more traction across ultra-low-power (ULP) embedded and cyber-physical systems, as it improves reliability, accuracy, and robustness under real-world constraints. However, adding more and more sensors to ultra-constrained sub-100 mW platforms requires balancing energy consumption against prediction accuracy. To achieve this ambitious goal, we present a novel adaptive AI methodology that combines multimodal sensor fusion (camera, ultrasound, and Time-of-Flight sensors) with a lightweight recurrent Transformer-based architecture (688 k parameters). We address the depth map estimation task with a mechanism that combines token propagation across iterations with incremental sensor utilization. At each iteration, a confidence-based gating mechanism dynamically decides whether to continue the computation by adding progressively richer but more power-demanding sensors as input. Token propagation ensures temporal consistency by forwarding context features across time. To deploy our algorithm and test a first real-world prototype, we design a novel printed circuit board featuring all three sensors, coupled with an ULP GWT GAP9 multicore System-on-Chip. When comparing our adaptive system against the same pipeline using all sensors and iterations on the NYUv2 dataset, we lose only 4.8% of the δ1 accuracy in exchange for 90% energy saving (2.44 mJ/frame). Finally, our adaptive method marks only 5.6% lower δ1 accuracy than MobileDepth despite using 9x fewer parameters. Compared with a state-of-the-art model also running on GAP9, our method improves δ1 accuracy by 31.8% thanks to our adaptive sensor fusion while operating within the same average power budget (~400 mW).

</details>


### [181] [Wavefront Parallelization for Efficient Learned Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19082)
*Shimon Murai,Fangzheng Lin,Kasidis Arunruangsirilert,Jiro Katto*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需重新训练的自回归图像压缩加速算法，通过波前并行推理实现13倍以上的解码加速，同时保持原有的率失真性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归上下文模型在图像压缩中表现优异，但其串行推理速度慢；现有加速方法（如棋盘格上下文）需要修改网络架构并重新训练，无法直接应用于预训练模型。

Method: 受视频编码标准中波前并行思想启发，将推理过程重新组织为最优的"交错"波前顺序，最小化串行步骤的同时保持精确的自回归依赖关系。

Result: 在预训练自回归模型（如Cheng等人模型）上实现超过13倍的加速，且保持原始率失真性能；通过权衡上下文依赖精度可进一步加速解码。

Conclusion: 提出了一种训练无关的推理加速算法，无需架构修改或重新训练即可显著加速预训练自回归图像压缩模型，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Autoregressive context models are foundational for learned image compression,but they suffer from slow serial inference. Existing acceleration methods such as checkerboard context require architectural changes and retraining, thus are inapplicable to pre-trained models. We propose a completely training-free inference-time acceleration algorithm inspired by wavefront parallelism in video coding standards. Our method reorganizes inference into an optimal ``staggered'' wavefront order, minimizing sequential steps while maintaining exact autoregressive dependencies. Experimental results show our approach accelerates pre-trained autoregressive models (e.g., Cheng et al.) by more than $13\times$ while preserving the original rate-distortion performance. We also demonstrate that faster decoding is possible by trading off precise context dependencies. Source code will be available at https://github.com/tokkiwa/compressai-wavefront.

</details>


### [182] [Fast reconstruction of tensor tomographic X-ray scattering data for real-time applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19124)
*André M. Antunes,Daniël M. Pelt,K. Joost Batenburg*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于X射线散射张量层析成像的直接重建方法，通过代数滤波器近似多次迭代更新，将计算时间从数分钟缩短至1秒，同时保持重建质量，为实时散射成像铺平了道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统X射线散射张量层析成像依赖缓慢的迭代重建，无法满足实时应用需求，亟需一种快速、稳定的直接重建方法。

Method: 针对平行束几何扩展直接重建方法：计算代数滤波器来近似多次迭代更新，通过单次滤波和反投影步骤完成重建；显式分离断层投影算符与视角依赖的混合算符，使方法可泛化至不同张量表示和散射测量模式。

Result: 模拟和实验结果表明，该方法在保持接近迭代重建质量的同时，计算速度提升一个数量级以上，可在商用硬件上1秒内完成53×53×53×28张量体积的重建。

Conclusion: 该方法的速度和稳定性支持高通量分析，为基于散射的实时成像提供了可能性。

Abstract: X-ray scattering tensor tomography reveals nanoscale structural orientation in 3D, but its reliance on slow iterative reconstruction limits real-time use. We introduce an extension of a direct reconstruction approach for parallel-beam geometries that computes algebraic filters approximating multiple iterative updates with a single filtering and back-projection step. By explicitly separating the tomographic projector from a view-dependent mixing operator, the method generalizes across tensor representations and modes of (small-angle) X-ray scattering measurement. Simulated and experimental results show that the approach approximates iterative reconstructions while reducing computation time by over an order of magnitude, realizing a reconstruction of a 53x53x53x28 tensor volume in 1 second on commercially-available hardware. The resulting speed and stability enable high-throughput analysis and open the door to real-time scattering-based imaging.

</details>


### [183] [MIRAGE: Multi-scale Lesion-Informed Representation with Auxiliary Guidance for MRI Contrast Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19137)
*Andrea Borghesi,Xin Wang,Jonas Teuwen,George Yiasemis*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: MIRAGE是一种残差2D U-Net，通过结合全局重建损失、感知损失以及三种仅在训练时可用的病灶感知监督（不对称肿瘤增强惩罚、多尺度辅助肿瘤分割、冻结的术后肿瘤分割nnU-Net引导），在乳腺MRI对比增强合成中显著优于pix2pix、条件扩散和潜桥匹配基线，在六个指标上排名第一，并揭示了保真度-效用之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单个平扫乳腺MRI切片推断对比增强图像是欠定问题，仅优化像素级保真度会抑制不确定的病灶增强，而生成式目标虽能产生逼真图像但无法保证患者特异性病灶保真度。

Method: 提出MIRAGE残差2D U-Net，联合使用全局重建损失和感知损失，并在训练时引入三种病灶感知监督：非对称肿瘤增强惩罚、多尺度辅助肿瘤分割、通过冻结的术后肿瘤分割nnU-Net提供引导。

Result: 在MAMA-SYNTH多中心数据集的301例病例上，使用八种互补指标评估，MIRAGE在六项指标中排名第一，在下游病灶定位上显著优于调优的pix2pix、条件扩散和潜桥匹配基线。

Conclusion: 任务感知合成方法虽能取得更优结果，但其最优性依赖于下游模型和用于定义效用的评估指标；不同损失函数在病灶定位上存在部分冗余，但对图像外观、影像组学和边界精度有不同影响。

Abstract: Inferring contrast enhancement from one pre-contrast breast MRI slice is underdetermined: post-contrast appearance contains physiological information that is not uniquely encoded in baseline anatomy. Optimizing only paired pixel fidelity can suppress uncertain lesion enhancement, whereas adversarial or stochastic generative objectives can favor realistic post-contrast appearance without guaranteeing patient-specific lesion fidelity. We introduce MIRAGE, a residual 2D U-Net that combines global reconstruction and perceptual losses with three forms of lesion-aware supervision available only during training: an asymmetric penalty for missed tumor enhancement, multi-scale auxiliary tumor segmentation, and guidance through a frozen post-contrast tumor segmentation nnU-Net. We evaluate the method on 301 cases from the multi-centre MAMA-SYNTH data using eight complementary image-, region-, radiomics-, and segmentation-based metrics. MIRAGE ranks first on six metrics and markedly improves downstream lesion localization over tuned pix2pix, conditional diffusion, and latent bridge-matching baselines. The generative alternatives retain advantages in LPIPS or contrast classification, revealing a clear fidelity-utility trade-off. Leave-one-in and leave-one-out ablations show that the losses are partly redundant for lesion localization but exert distinct effects on appearance, radiomics, and boundary accuracy. These results support task-aware synthesis while also showing that its apparent optimality is conditional on the downstream models and metrics used to define utility.

</details>
